  try {
    if (window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone) return;
    window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone = true;

    var path = (window.location.pathname || '/').split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

100%)"></span><style data-custom-css-index="0" data-custom-css-path="custom.css">.ultra-hero {
  background-color: #00CFEB;
  color: #012A33;
  padding: 32px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  margin: 24px 0;
}

.ultra-hero h2 {
  color: #012A33;
  margin-top: 0;
  font-size: 24px;
}

.ultra-hero p {
  font-size: 16px;
  line-height: 1.6;
  color: #012A33;
}

.ultra-hero a {
  color: #012A33;
  text-decoration: underline;
  font-weight: 600;
}

/* Beta tag pill in sidebar - increase font size */
[class*="sidebar"] [class*="tag"],
[class*="sidebar"] [class*="badge"],
nav [class*="tag"],
nav [class*="badge"] {
  font-size: 0.8rem !important;
}

/* Keep the OneTrust cookie preferences control clear of the left sidebar. */
#ot-sdk-btn-floating {
  left: auto !important;
  right: 10px !important;
}

/* Keep the preview-only widget and cookie preferences control from colliding. */
body:has([aria-label="Preview Widget"]) #ot-sdk-btn-floating {
  bottom: 88px !important;
}

/*
 * Fundamentals is a routed collection, not a product tab. Mintlify reveals a
 * hidden tab when one of its pages is active, so keep its unique tab control
 * out of the product navigation in that state as well.
 */
#navbar .nav-tabs-item:has(> div > svg[style*="/book-open.svg"]) {
  display: none;
}

/* Mini-site tab rows show only the active tab, from first paint: the server
   marks it with aria-current="location", so pruning needs no JS.
   home-directory.js only re-syncs inline state across SPA navigations. */
.nav-tabs a.nav-tabs-item:not([aria-current="location"]) { display: none; }

/* Single-tab mini-sites (Other APIs, Agent SDK, Networking) get a tab row
   whose one tab just repeats the product name — a redundant "⋯ Other APIs"
   band under the navbar. Hide the row's container (the row itself sits in
   a fixed-height wrapper, so hiding .nav-tabs alone would leave an empty
   band) when the row holds exactly one authored tab. Tab anchors are the
   row's only children, so :first-child:last-child counts authored tabs;
   multi-tab rows keep their pruned active-tab title. */
#navbar div:has(> div > .nav-tabs > a.nav-tabs-item:first-child:last-child) {
  display: none;
}

/* Desktop homepage uses the directory; section pages use native sidebars. */
@media (width >= 64rem) {
  .txd-side { display: block; }
  button[data-component-part="products-selector-trigger"],
  button.tx-product-switcher { display: none !important; }
}
/* Match desktop's absent stock switcher without hiding the native Home link. */
#mobile-nav [data-component-part="products-selector-trigger"],
#mobile-nav #navigation-items > div:has(> div > button[aria-haspopup="menu"]),
#mobile-nav.tx-has-directory #navigation-items {
  display: none !important;
}
#mobile-nav .tx-mobile-directory { padding: 0; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; }
#mobile-nav .tx-mobile-directory a,
#mobile-nav .tx-mobile-directory .tx-dir-suite-head {
  min-height: 2.75rem;
}
#mobile-nav .tx-mobile-directory :is(a, .tx-dir-suite-head):focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid currentColor; outline-offset: -2px;
}

/* --- Home directory ------------------------------------------------------
   Server-rendered into docs/overview.mdx (mode: custom) between the tx-tree
   markers by scripts/generate-home-directory.js. The links are present in the
   initial HTML; home-directory.js adds progressive disclosure on desktop.
   The .txd-* classes lay out the custom page. */
/* Geometry mirrors the native mini-site layout so the sidebar doesn't
   jump when crossing home <-> product pages: native #navigation-items sits
   flush left at 18rem wide and the content column starts 476px in
   (measured at 1600px). */
.txd-shell {
  display: flex; gap: 0; max-width: none;
  margin: 0; padding: 3rem 2rem 6rem 0;
}
.txd-side { width: 18rem; flex-shrink: 0; }
.txd-main { flex: 1; min-width: 0; max-width: 56rem; margin-left: 11.75rem; }
@media (max-width: 80rem) {
  .txd-main { margin-left: 4rem; }
}
@media (width < 64rem) {
  .txd-shell { display: block; padding: 2rem 1.5rem 5rem; }
  .txd-side { display: none; }
  .txd-main { margin-left: 0; }
}

/* CF-style home directory (shared by the static tree) */
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) { margin: 0 0 0.5rem; padding: 0 0.5rem 0 1rem; }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-label {
  font-size: 0.72rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  text-transform: uppercase; opacity: 0.55; margin: 0 0 0.4rem;
}
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-label-gap { margin-top: 1.5rem; }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-link,
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) summary {
  display: flex; align-items: center; cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 0.875rem; line-height: 1.25rem; padding: 0.35rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 0.375rem; color: rgb(55 65 81); text-decoration: none;
}
html.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-link,
html.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) summary { color: rgb(156 163 175); }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-link:hover,
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) summary:hover { color: rgb(17 24 39); background: rgb(0 0 0 / 0.04); }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-link[aria-current="page"] {
  color: #00b688; background: transparent; box-shadow: none; font-weight: 400;
  text-shadow: -0.2px 0 0 currentColor, 0.2px 0 0 currentColor;
}
html.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-link:hover,
html.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) summary:hover { color: rgb(229 231 235); background: rgb(255 255 255 / 0.06); }
html.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-link[aria-current="page"] {
  color: #5eead4; background: transparent; box-shadow: none;
}
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite { margin-bottom: 0.15rem; }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite-head {
  display: flex; align-items: center; cursor: pointer; user-select: none;
  font-size: 0.875rem; line-height: 1.25rem; font-weight: 500;
  padding: 0.35rem 0.5rem; border-radius: 0.375rem; color: rgb(55 65 81);
}
html.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite-head { color: rgb(156 163 175); }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite-head:hover { color: rgb(17 24 39); background: rgb(0 0 0 / 0.04); }
html.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite-head:hover { color: rgb(229 231 235); background: rgb(255 255 255 / 0.06); }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite-head::after {
  content: ''; width: 0.42rem; height: 0.42rem; margin-left: auto;
  border-right: 1.5px solid currentColor; border-bottom: 1.5px solid currentColor;
  transform: rotate(-45deg); transition: transform 0.15s; flex-shrink: 0; opacity: 0.7;
}
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite.open > .tx-dir-suite-head::after { transform: rotate(45deg); }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite.open > .tx-dir-suite-head {
  color: rgb(17 24 39); background: rgb(0 0 0 / 0.04); font-weight: 600;
}
html.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite.open > .tx-dir-suite-head {
  color: rgb(229 231 235); background: rgb(255 255 255 / 0.06);
}
/* Collapsed by default at first paint; home-directory.js toggles .open.
   Links stay in the served HTML either way, so crawlers see everything. */
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite > ul { display: none; }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite.open > ul { display: block; }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) ul { margin: 0.15rem 0 0.5rem; padding: 0; }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) li { list-style: none; }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) li a {
  display: flex; align-items: center; font-size: 0.875rem; line-height: 1.25rem;
  padding: 0.3rem 0.5rem 0.3rem 1.6rem; border-radius: 0.375rem;
  color: rgb(107 114 128); text-decoration: none;
}
html.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) li a { color: rgb(156 163 175); }
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) li a:hover { color: rgb(17 24 39); background: rgb(0 0 0 / 0.04); }
html.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) li a:hover { color: rgb(229 231 235); background: rgb(255 255 255 / 0.06); }
/* Font Awesome glyphs from the docs.json tab icons, masked so they inherit
   each row's text color in both themes (same technique as the native nav). */
:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-icon {
  display: inline-block; width: 1rem; height: 1rem;
  flex-shrink: 0; margin-right: 0.5rem;
  background-color: currentColor;
  -webkit-mask-repeat: no-repeat; mask-repeat: no-repeat;
  -webkit-mask-position: center; mask-position: center;
  -webkit-mask-size: contain; mask-size: contain;
}

/* Icon glyph classes for the server-rendered directory tree. */
/* tx-icons:start — derived from docs.json; run `npm run generate:home-dir` */
.txi-bolt { -webkit-mask-image: url("https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/bolt.svg"); mask-image: url("https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/bolt.svg"); }

.txi-message-dots { -webkit-mask-image: url("https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/message-dots.svg"); mask-image: url("https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/message-dots.svg"); }

.txi-server { -webkit-mask-image: url("https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/server.svg"); mask-image: url("https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/server.svg"); }

.txi-waveform { -webkit-mask-image: url("https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/waveform.svg"); mask-image: url("https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/waveform.svg"); }
.txi-waveform-lines { -webkit-mask-image: url("https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/waveform-lines.svg"); mask-image: url("https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/regular/waveform-lines.svg"); }
/* tx-icons:end */

/* Typography for the mode:custom home canvas (markdown is not compiled
   inside the layout wrappers, so headings/paragraphs there are raw JSX). */
.txd-main .txd-h1 { font-family: var(--font-family-headings-custom, "PP Formula"), -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", system-ui, sans-serif; font-size: 2.1rem; font-weight: 500; letter-spacing: -0.01em; margin: 1.2rem 0 0.4rem; color: rgb(22 28 26); }
html.dark .txd-main .txd-h1 { color: rgb(229 231 235); }
.txd-main .txd-lead { font-size: 1.3rem; line-height: 1.55; font-weight: 500; margin: 0.9rem 0 1.3rem; color: rgb(31 41 55); }
html.dark .txd-main .txd-lead { color: rgb(209 213 219); }
.txd-main .txd-h2 { font-family: var(--font-family-headings-custom, "PP Formula"), -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", system-ui, sans-serif; font-size: 1.45rem; font-weight: 500; margin: 2.4rem 0 0.4rem; color: rgb(22 28 26); }
html.dark .txd-main .txd-h2 { color: rgb(229 231 235); }
.txd-main .txd-section-h { font-size: 1.05rem; font-weight: 600; margin: 1.8rem 0 0.2rem; opacity: 0.85; }
.txd-main .txd-p { margin: 0.7rem 0 1rem; line-height: 1.65; color: rgb(75 85 99); }
html.dark .txd-main .txd-p { color: rgb(156 163 175); }
.txd-main .txd-p a { text-decoration: underline; }

/* Homepage content uses Mintlify's native Card, Callout, and CodeGroup
   components. Only the custom canvas typography and spacing live here. */
.txd-main .txd-h1 {
  max-width: 46rem;
  line-height: 1.12;
}
.txd-main .txd-hero-copy { max-width: 45rem; }
.txd-main .txd-follow-up { margin-top: 1rem; }
.txd-main .txd-follow-up a {
  color: #00b688;
  font-weight: 600;
  text-decoration: none;
}
.txd-main .txd-follow-up a:hover { text-decoration: underline; }
.txd-main a:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid #00b688;
  outline-offset: 3px;
}
@media (max-width: 48rem) {
  .txd-main .txd-h1 { font-size: 1.9rem; }
}
@media (min-width: 64.01rem) and (max-width: 90rem) {
  .txd-main { margin-right: 4rem; }
}

/* Agent SDK ownership comparison on the overview page. */
.agent-sdk-ownership-matrix {
  --matrix-border: #dbe4e1;
  --matrix-panel: #ffffff;
  --matrix-soft: #f7faf9;
  --matrix-text: #17201e;
  --matrix-muted: #64748b;
  --matrix-you-bg: #eef2f6;
  --matrix-you-text: #334155;
  --matrix-telnyx-bg: #d8faf0;
  --matrix-telnyx-text: #08785d;
  margin: 1.5rem 0 2rem;
}

.dark .agent-sdk-ownership-matrix {
  --matrix-border: #29343a;
  --matrix-panel: #11171b;
  --matrix-soft: #0e1418;
  --matrix-text: #e7efec;
  --matrix-muted: #9ca3af;
  --matrix-you-bg: #26313a;
  --matrix-you-text: #dbe4e8;
  --matrix-telnyx-bg: #103d34;
  --matrix-telnyx-text: #5eead4;
}

.agent-sdk-ownership-scroll {
  overflow-x: auto;
  padding: 1px 1px 8px;
}

.agent-sdk-ownership-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(126px, .82fr) repeat(3, minmax(156px, 1fr));
  min-width: 640px;
  overflow: hidden;
  color: var(--matrix-text);
  background: var(--matrix-panel);
  border: 1px solid var(--matrix-border);
  border-radius: 16px;
  box-shadow: 0 12px 35px rgb(15 23 42 / 7%);
}

.dark .agent-sdk-ownership-grid {
  box-shadow: 0 16px 40px rgb(0 0 0 / 20%);
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-row {
  display: contents;
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-row > * {
  border-right: 1px solid var(--matrix-border);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--matrix-border);
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-row > *:last-child {
  border-right: 0;
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-row-last > * {
  border-bottom: 0;
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-corner,
.agent-sdk-matrix-way {
  min-height: 118px;
  padding: 20px 18px 17px;
  background: var(--matrix-soft);
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-corner {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-end;
  color: var(--matrix-muted);
  font-size: .74rem;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: .02em;
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-way {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 5px;
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-way::before {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  right: 0;
  left: 0;
  height: 4px;
  content: "";
  background: var(--way-color);
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-way.way-one { --way-color: #f28c3a; }
.agent-sdk-matrix-way.way-two { --way-color: #18b99b; }
.agent-sdk-matrix-way.way-three { --way-color: #a875f5; }

.agent-sdk-way-number {
  color: var(--way-color);
  font-size: .72rem;
  font-weight: 850;
  letter-spacing: .03em;
}

.agent-sdk-way-title {
  font-family: "PP Formula", -apple-system, "system-ui", "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
  color: var(--matrix-text);
  font-size: .94rem;
  font-weight: 800;
  line-height: 1.25;
}

.agent-sdk-way-caption {
  color: var(--matrix-muted);
  font-size: .72rem;
  font-weight: 650;
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-layer,
.agent-sdk-matrix-owner {
  min-height: 64px;
  padding: 14px 18px;
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-layer {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  font-family: "PP Formula", -apple-system, "system-ui", "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
  color: var(--matrix-text);
  background: var(--matrix-soft);
  font-size: .88rem;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-owner {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  background: var(--matrix-panel);
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-owner span {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  padding: 6px 10px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: .72rem;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: .02em;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-owner span::before {
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  content: "";
  background: currentColor;
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-owner .owner-you {
  color: var(--matrix-you-text);
  background: var(--matrix-you-bg);
}

.agent-sdk-matrix-owner .owner-telnyx {
  color: var(--matrix-telnyx-text);
  background: var(--matrix-telnyx-bg);
}

.agent-sdk-shared-foundation {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(126px, .82fr) 1fr auto;
  min-width: 640px;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 18px;
  margin-top: 10px;
  padding: 14px 18px;
  color: #0b5e4d;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #dff8f0, #eefbf7);
  border: 1px solid #8ed8c4;
  border-radius: 13px;
}

.dark .agent-sdk-shared-foundation {
  color: #99f6e4;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #10352f, #102521);
  border-color: #247865;
}

.agent-sdk-shared-foundation span {
  font-size: .7rem;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: .02em;
}

.agent-sdk-shared-foundation span:last-child {
  color: var(--matrix-muted);
  font-size: .64rem;
}

.agent-sdk-foundation-name {
  font-family: "PP Formula", -apple-system, "system-ui", "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
  color: inherit;
  font-size: .94rem;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0;
  text-transform: none;
}

/* Search-first directory on generated SDK resource-map pages. */
.rm-explorer {
  --rm-border: rgb(15 23 42 / 14%);
  --rm-muted: #5f6b68;
  --rm-soft: #f6f8f7;
}

.dark .rm-explorer {
  --rm-border: rgb(255 255 255 / 14%);
  --rm-muted: #a2aaa8;
  --rm-soft: #151a19;
}

#resource-map-toolbar {
  position: sticky;
  top: 5rem;
  z-index: 20;
  margin: 1.25rem 0 1.75rem;
  padding: .75rem 0;
  background: var(--rm-surface, #fff);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rm-border);
}

.dark #resource-map-toolbar {
  background: #0b0e0d;
}

.rm-controls {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(15rem, 1fr) minmax(10rem, auto) auto;
  gap: .625rem;
  align-items: center;
}

.rm-search {
  position: relative;
  min-width: 0;
}

.rm-search svg {
  position: absolute;
  top: 50%;
  left: .75rem;
  width: 1rem;
  height: 1rem;
  color: var(--rm-muted);
  fill: none;
  stroke: currentColor;
  stroke-width: 1.8;
  transform: translateY(-50%);
  pointer-events: none;
}

.rm-search input,
.rm-area-select,
.rm-collapse,
.rm-clear,
.rm-empty button {
  min-height: 2.5rem;
  color: inherit;
  background: transparent;
  border: 1px solid var(--rm-border);
  border-radius: .5rem;
  font: inherit;
  font-size: .875rem;
}

.rm-search input {
  width: 100%;
  padding: .58rem 2.6rem .58rem 2.35rem;
}

.rm-search input::placeholder {
  color: var(--rm-muted);
  opacity: 1;
}

.rm-shortcut {
  position: absolute;
  top: 50%;
  right: .65rem;
  display: grid;
  width: 1.5rem;
  height: 1.5rem;
  place-items: center;
  color: var(--rm-muted);
  background: var(--rm-soft);
  border: 1px solid var(--rm-border);
  border-radius: .35rem;
  font: inherit;
  font-size: .72rem;
  transform: translateY(-50%);
  pointer-events: none;
}

.rm-area-wrap {
  position: relative;
  max-width: 13rem;
}

.rm-area-select {
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 13rem;
  padding: .55rem 2.25rem .55rem .75rem;
  appearance: none;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.rm-area-caret {
  position: absolute;
  top: 50%;
  right: .85rem;
  width: .45rem;
  height: .45rem;
  border-right: 1.5px solid currentColor;
  border-bottom: 1.5px solid currentColor;
  transform: translateY(-70%) rotate(45deg);
  pointer-events: none;
}

.rm-collapse,
.rm-clear,
.rm-empty button {
  padding: .5rem .75rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.rm-search input:focus-visible,
.rm-area-select:focus-visible,
.rm-collapse:focus-visible,
.rm-clear:focus-visible,
.rm-empty button:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid #00b688;
  outline-offset: 2px;
  border-color: #00b688;
  box-shadow: none !important;
}

.rm-collapse:hover,
.rm-clear:hover,
.rm-empty button:hover {
  color: #08785d;
  background: var(--rm-soft);
}

.dark .rm-collapse:hover,
.dark .rm-clear:hover,
.dark .rm-empty button:hover {
  color: #5eead4;
}

.rm-meta {
  display: flex;
  min-height: 1.65rem;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: .75rem;
  padding: .45rem .15rem 0;
}

.rm-status {
  color: var(--rm-muted);
  font-size: .78rem;
  line-height: 1.35;
}

.rm-clear {
  min-height: 1.8rem;
  padding: .2rem .55rem;
  border-color: transparent;
  font-size: .78rem;
}

.rm-empty {
  display: grid;
  justify-items: start;
  gap: .35rem;
  margin: -.5rem 0 1.75rem;
  padding: 1.1rem 1.2rem;
  color: var(--rm-muted);
  background: var(--rm-soft);
  border: 1px solid var(--rm-border);
  border-radius: .75rem;
  font-size: .875rem;
}

.rm-empty strong {
  color: inherit;
  font-size: .95rem;
}

.rm-empty button {
  min-height: 2.2rem;
  margin-top: .35rem;
}

.rm-resource-list {
  display: block !important;
  margin: 0 0 .75rem !important;
  border-color: var(--rm-border) !important;
  border-top: 0 !important;
  border-radius: 0 0 .75rem .75rem !important;
}

.rm-resource-list > details.accordion {
  margin: 0 !important;
  border: 0 !important;
  border-radius: 0 !important;
}

.rm-resource-list > details.accordion + details.accordion {
  border-top: 1px solid var(--rm-border) !important;
}

.rm-resource-list > details.accordion > summary {
  min-height: 3rem;
  padding: .7rem .9rem !important;
  border-radius: 0 !important;
}

.rm-resource-list > details.accordion:first-child > summary {
  border-radius: 0 !important;
}

.rm-resource-list > details.accordion:last-child:not([open]) > summary {
  border-radius: 0 0 .75rem .75rem !important;
}

.rm-resource-list [data-component-part="accordion-title"] {
  display: flex;
  min-width: 0;
  align-items: baseline;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1rem;
}

.rm-resource-name {
  min-width: 0;
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
  font-weight: 650;
}

.rm-method-count {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  color: var(--rm-muted);
  font-size: .75rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.rm-resource-list details[open] > summary {
  background: var(--rm-soft);
}

.rm-resource-list details[open] [data-component-part="accordion-content"] {
  margin-right: 1rem;
  margin-left: 1rem;
}

.rm-resource-list tbody tr.rm-row-match {
  background: rgb(0 182 136 / 8%);
}

.rm-resource-list td:first-child code {
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.rm-resource-list tbody tr[hidden],
.rm-explorer h2[hidden],
.rm-resource-list[hidden],
.rm-empty[hidden],
.rm-collapse[hidden],
.rm-clear[hidden] {
  display: none !important;
}

.rm-explorer h2 {
  position: relative;
  min-height: 3rem;
  margin: .45rem 0 0 !important;
  scroll-margin-top: 10rem;
  border: 1px solid var(--rm-border);
  border-radius: .75rem;
}

.rm-explorer h2.rm-section-open {
  border-radius: .75rem .75rem 0 0;
}

.rm-section-toggle {
  display: grid;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 3rem;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto auto;
  align-items: center;
  gap: .75rem;
  padding: .6rem .9rem;
  color: inherit;
  text-align: left;
  background: transparent;
  border: 0;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.rm-section-toggle:hover,
.rm-section-toggle:focus-visible {
  background: var(--rm-soft);
}

.rm-section-toggle:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid #00b688;
  outline-offset: 2px;
  border-radius: .7rem;
}

.rm-section-name {
  font-size: 1rem;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.rm-section-count {
  display: grid;
  min-width: 1.65rem;
  min-height: 1.4rem;
  place-items: center;
  padding: 3px 6px;
  color: #08785d;
  background: rgb(0 182 136 / 10%);
  border-radius: .35rem;
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  line-height: 16px;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.dark .rm-section-count {
  color: #5eead4;
  background: #12302b;
}

.rm-section-caret {
  width: .5rem;
  height: .5rem;
  border-right: 1.5px solid currentColor;
  border-bottom: 1.5px solid currentColor;
  transform: rotate(45deg) translateY(-.15rem);
}

.rm-section-toggle[aria-expanded="true"] .rm-section-caret {
  transform: rotate(225deg) translate(-.1rem, -.05rem);
}

@media (max-width: 767px) {
  #resource-map-toolbar {
    position: static;
    top: auto;
    z-index: auto;
    margin-top: 1rem;
    padding-top: .6rem;
  }

  .rm-controls {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  }

  .rm-search {
    grid-column: 1 / -1;
  }

  .rm-search input {
    padding-right: .75rem;
  }

  .rm-shortcut {
    display: none;
  }

  .rm-area-wrap,
  .rm-area-select {
    width: 100%;
    max-width: none;
  }

  .rm-resource-list [data-component-part="accordion-title"] {
    gap: .65rem;
  }

  .rm-method-count {
    font-size: .7rem;
  }

  /* Method and endpoint pairs must remain readable without a hidden horizontal
     swipe gesture. Preserve the semantic table on larger screens, then stack
     each row as a labeled record on narrow viewports. */
  .rm-resource-list table,
  .rm-resource-list tbody {
    display: block;
    width: 100%;
    min-width: 0 !important;
  }

  .rm-resource-list thead {
    display: none;
  }

  .rm-resource-list tbody tr {
    display: grid;
    width: 100%;
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
    gap: .45rem;
    padding: .75rem 0;
  }

  .rm-resource-list tbody tr + tr {
    border-top: 1px solid var(--rm-border);
  }

  .rm-resource-list tbody td {
    display: grid;
    width: 100%;
    min-width: 0 !important;
    grid-template-columns: 4.5rem minmax(0, 1fr);
    align-items: start;
    gap: .5rem;
    padding: 0 !important;
    border: 0 !important;
  }

  .rm-resource-list tbody td::before {
    color: var(--rm-muted);
    font-size: .68rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: .04em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }

  .rm-resource-list tbody td:first-child::before {
    content: "Method";
  }

  .rm-resource-list tbody td:last-child::before {
    content: "Endpoint";
  }

  .rm-resource-list tbody td code,
  .rm-resource-list tbody td a {
    min-width: 0;
    white-space: normal !important;
    overflow-wrap: anywhere;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .rm-explorer *,
  .rm-explorer *::before,
  .rm-explorer *::after {
    scroll-behavior: auto !important;
    transition-duration: .01ms !important;
  }
}

/* Task-first launcher on the server-side SDK landing page. */
.sdk-launchpad {
  --sdk-border: #d8e1de;
  --sdk-panel: #ffffff;
  margin: 1rem 0 1.5rem;
  padding: 1rem;
  overflow: hidden;
  color: inherit;
  background: var(--sdk-panel);
  border: 1px solid var(--sdk-border);
  border-radius: 12px;
}

.dark .sdk-launchpad {
  --sdk-border: #293431;
  --sdk-panel: #101513;
}

.sdk-launchpad-kicker {
  margin: 0 0 .25rem !important;
  color: #08785d;
  font-size: .68rem;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: .1em;
}

.dark .sdk-launchpad-kicker { color: #5eead4; }

.sdk-launchpad > h2 {
  margin-top: .25rem;
}

.sdk-launchpad [role="tablist"] {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: .25rem;
}

.sdk-launchpad [role="tab"] {
  min-width: 56px;
  min-height: 44px;
}

.sdk-launchpad [role="tab"][aria-selected="true"] {
  color: #08785d !important;
}

.dark .sdk-launchpad [role="tab"][aria-selected="true"] {
  color: #5eead4 !important;
}

.sdk-launchpad [role="tab"]:focus-visible,
.sdk-launchpad a:focus-visible,
.sdk-launchpad-code:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid #08785d;
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

.dark .sdk-launchpad [role="tab"]:focus-visible,
.dark .sdk-launchpad a:focus-visible,
.dark .sdk-launchpad-code:focus-visible {
  outline-color: #5eead4;
}

.sdk-launchpad-code {
  max-width: 100%;
  overflow-x: auto;
  border-radius: .5rem;
}

.sdk-launchpad-code pre {
  max-width: none;
  overflow-x: visible !important;
}

.sdk-launchpad-code button {
  min-width: 44px;
  min-height: 44px;
}

/* The sticky composer covers quickstart code; the header assistant remains available. */
#content-area:has(#sdk-launchpad) .chat-assistant-floating-input {
  display: none;
}

@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .sdk-launchpad {
    margin-top: .75rem;
    padding: .75rem;
  }

  .sdk-launchpad-kicker {
    display: none;
  }

  .sdk-launchpad [role="tablist"] {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(6, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: .2rem;
    overflow: visible;
  }

  .sdk-launchpad [role="tab"] {
    min-width: 0;
    min-height: 44px;
    padding-inline: .2rem;
    font-size: .75rem;
    line-height: 1.15;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
}

.sdk-directory li {
  margin-block: .5rem;
}

/*
 * Mintlify's stock prose table floors every cell at 150px
 * (`[&_td]:min-w-[150px]`), so a 5+ column table cannot fit the ~700px docs
 * content column: it renders at its 150px-per-cell minimum and clips behind a
 * scrollbar-less horizontal scroll strip — e.g. the model comparison on
 * /docs/voice/stt/models needs 900px inside a 737px column. Let cells shrink
 * below that floor and wrap long description tokens so authored tables
 * fit the column. Tables that already fit are unaffected: the floor only
 * ever widens them.
 */
.mdx-content table td {
  min-width: 0 !important;
}

.mdx-content table td :is(code, a) {
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
}

/* Field identifiers need their intrinsic width; descriptions can still wrap.
   Mintlify's scrollable table wrapper handles tables wider than the page. */
.mdx-content table :is(th, td):first-child code {
  white-space: nowrap;
  word-break: normal;
  overflow-wrap: normal;
}

linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Network Connectivity Requirements</span></h1>
<span data-as="p">For the Telnyx WebRTC JS SDK to function properly, the client must be able to reach Telnyx’s signaling and media infrastructure.</span>
<hr/>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="overview"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#overview" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Overview</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The SDK requires connectivity to three types of endpoints:</span>
<!--$!--><template data-dgst="BAILOUT_TO_CLIENT_SIDE_RENDERING"></template><!--/$-->
<hr/>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="signaling"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#signaling" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Signaling</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The SDK uses a persistent WebSocket connection for call signaling (invite, answer, hangup, etc.).</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_4pamqqnpfb39p7b9snpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Property</th><th>Value</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Host</strong></td><td><code>rtc.telnyx.com</code></td></tr><tr><td><strong>Port</strong></td><td data-numeric="true">443</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Protocol</strong></td><td>WSS (WebSocket Secure / TLS)</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Direction</strong></td><td>Outbound</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Requirements:</strong></span>
<ul>
<li>Outbound WebSocket connections must be allowed on port 443</li>
<li>No HTTP long-polling fallback — WebSocket is required</li>
<li>Connection must remain open for the duration of the session</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Custom endpoint:</strong> You can override the signaling server using <a class="link" href="/docs/development/webrtc/js-sdk/reference/iclientoptions">IClientOptions</a> <code>env</code> property, but this is not recommended for production.</span>
<hr/>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="stun"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#stun" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">STUN</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">STUN servers help the client discover its public IP address for ICE negotiation.</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_89amqqnpfb39p7b9snpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Property</th><th>Value</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Primary</strong></td><td><code>stun.telnyx.com:3478</code></td></tr><tr><td><strong>Fallback</strong></td><td><code>stun.l.google.com:19302</code></td></tr><tr><td><strong>Protocol</strong></td><td>UDP</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Direction</strong></td><td>Outbound</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The SDK automatically uses these STUN servers. No configuration required.</span>
<hr/>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="turn"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#turn" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">TURN</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">TURN servers relay media when direct peer-to-peer connectivity is not possible (e.g., symmetric NAT, restrictive firewalls).</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_apamqqnpfb39p7b9snpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Property</th><th>Value</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Host (UDP/TCP)</strong></td><td><code>turn.telnyx.com</code></td></tr><tr><td><strong>Host (TURNS)</strong></td><td><code>turn2.telnyx.com</code></td></tr><tr><td><strong>Port (UDP)</strong></td><td data-numeric="true">3478</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Port (TCP)</strong></td><td data-numeric="true">3478</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Port (TURNS)</strong></td><td data-numeric="true">443</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Protocol</strong></td><td>UDP (preferred) / TCP (fallback) / TLS over 443 (last resort)</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Authentication</strong></td><td>Automatic (long-term credentials)</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Direction</strong></td><td>Outbound</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The SDK automatically provisions TURN credentials. No manual configuration required.</span>
<span data-as="p"><strong>UDP vs TCP vs TURNS/443:</strong></span>
<ul>
<li><strong>UDP</strong> (preferred) — Lower latency, better for real-time audio</li>
<li><strong>TCP</strong> (fallback) — Higher latency, used when UDP is blocked</li>
<li><strong>TURNS over 443</strong> (last resort) — TURN over TLS on port 443, used when both UDP and TCP/3478 are blocked by restrictive firewalls or proxies</li>
</ul>
<div class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-zinc-500/20 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:border-zinc-500/30 dark:bg-zinc-500/10" data-callout-type="callout"><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">TURN credentials are automatically provisioned by the SDK. You do not need to configure TURN usernames or passwords.</span></div></div>
<div class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-zinc-500/20 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:border-zinc-500/30 dark:bg-zinc-500/10" data-callout-type="callout"><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">Starting with SDK v2.27.4, the default ICE server list includes a TURNS (TURN over TLS) entry on port 443 (<code>turns:turn2.telnyx.com:443</code>) in addition to the existing TURN UDP/3478 and TCP/3478 entries. This provides a last-resort relay fallback for networks that block both UDP/3478 and TCP/3478 but allow outbound TCP/443.</span></div></div>
<hr/>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="firewall-configuration"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#firewall-configuration" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Firewall Configuration</span></h2>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="minimum-required-rules"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#minimum-required-rules" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Minimum required rules</span></h3>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_f9amqqnpfb39p7b9snpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Direction</th><th>Destination</th><th>Port</th><th>Protocol</th><th>Purpose</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Outbound</td><td><code>rtc.telnyx.com</code></td><td data-numeric="true">443</td><td>WSS</td><td>Signaling</td></tr><tr><td>Outbound</td><td><code>stun.telnyx.com</code></td><td data-numeric="true">3478</td><td>UDP</td><td>STUN</td></tr><tr><td>Outbound</td><td><code>turn.telnyx.com</code></td><td data-numeric="true">3478</td><td>UDP</td><td>TURN (media relay, preferred)</td></tr><tr><td>Outbound</td><td><code>turn.telnyx.com</code></td><td data-numeric="true">3478</td><td>TCP</td><td>TURN (fallback)</td></tr><tr><td>Outbound</td><td><code>turn2.telnyx.com</code></td><td data-numeric="true">443</td><td>TLS</td><td>TURN over TLS (restrictive-network fallback)</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="optional-but-recommended"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#optional-but-recommended" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Optional but recommended</span></h3>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_g9amqqnpfb39p7b9snpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Direction</th><th>Destination</th><th>Port</th><th>Protocol</th><th>Purpose</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Outbound</td><td><code>stun.l.google.com</code></td><td data-numeric="true">19302</td><td>UDP</td><td>STUN fallback</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="media-ports"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#media-ports" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Media ports</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">RTP media uses dynamic ports allocated by the browser. These are ephemeral and cannot be whitelisted by port number. Instead:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Ensure TURN is accessible</strong> — TURN handles media relay when direct connectivity fails</li>
<li><strong>Allow UDP outbound</strong> to Telnyx media servers (the <code>remote_media_ip</code> seen in SDP)</li>
<li><strong>Don’t restrict outbound UDP to specific ports</strong> — this will break WebRTC</li>
</ul>
<hr/>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="restrictive-network-scenarios"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#restrictive-network-scenarios" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Restrictive Network Scenarios</span></h2>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="stun-fails-error-701"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#stun-fails-error-701" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">STUN fails (error 701)</span></h3>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Symptom:</strong> Client cannot discover its public IP. No <code>srflx</code> or <code>prflx</code> ICE candidates.</span>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Fix:</strong></span>
<ol>
<li>Check firewall allows UDP to <code>stun.telnyx.com:3478</code></li>
<li>If STUN is blocked, TURN may still work — the SDK falls back automatically</li>
<li>If both STUN and TURN are blocked, calls cannot connect</li>
</ol>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="turn-fails"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#turn-fails" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">TURN fails</span></h3>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Symptom:</strong> Client is on a restrictive network (symmetric NAT), can’t get <code>relay</code> candidates.</span>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Fix:</strong></span>
<ol>
<li>Check firewall allows UDP to <code>turn.telnyx.com:3478</code></li>
<li>If UDP is blocked, check firewall allows TCP to <code>turn.telnyx.com:3478</code></li>
<li>If both UDP/3478 and TCP/3478 are blocked, TURNS over TLS on port 443 to <code>turn2.telnyx.com</code> will work — this is included in the default ICE server list since SDK v2.27.4</li>
<li>If all TURN paths are blocked, use <code>forceRelayCandidate: true</code> to skip direct connectivity attempts:</li>
</ol>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span data-as="p">Starting with SDK v2.27.4, the SDK exports a public <code>TELNYX_ICE_SERVERS</code> catalog of ready-to-use ICE server entries. Import it and compose any combination into the <code>iceServers</code> option to override the defaults:</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="corporate-vpn"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#corporate-vpn" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Corporate VPN</span></h3>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Symptom:</strong> Calls fail or have poor quality through VPN.</span>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Fix:</strong></span>
<ol>
<li>Whitelist <code>rtc.telnyx.com</code>, <code>stun.telnyx.com</code>, <code>turn.telnyx.com</code>, and <code>turn2.telnyx.com</code> in VPN split-tunneling config</li>
<li>Ensure VPN doesn’t block UDP traffic to TURN servers or TLS to <code>turn2.telnyx.com:443</code></li>
<li>Consider split-tunneling so WebRTC traffic bypasses the VPN</li>
</ol>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="docker-/-container-environments"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#docker-/-container-environments" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Docker / Container environments</span></h3>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Symptom:</strong> STUN errors, no ICE candidates, one-way audio.</span>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Fix:</strong></span>
<ol>
<li>Docker’s default bridge network (<code>172.x</code> or <code>10.x</code>) can interfere with ICE candidate gathering</li>
<li>Use <code>--network host</code> mode for the container</li>
<li>Or configure the Docker network to use the host’s network stack</li>
</ol>
<hr/>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-medium" id="debug-tools"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#debug-tools" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Debug tools</span></h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>SDK debug mode:</strong> Set <code>debug: true</code> and <code>debugOutput: &#x27;socket&#x27;</code> in <a class="link" href="/docs/development/webrtc/js-sdk/reference/iclientoptions">IClientOptions</a></li>
<li><strong>Debug visualizer:</strong> Upload debug data to <code>https://webrtc-debug.telnyx.com/</code></li>
<li><strong>Call reports:</strong> Enable <code>enableCallReports: true</code> for programmatic access to ICE stats</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">See <a class="link" href="/docs/development/webrtc/js-sdk/how-to/debug-call-issues">Debug Data &amp; Call Quality Analysis</a> for interpreting debug output.</span>
<hr/>

Typical: ~30 kbps</td></tr><tr><td>PCMU (G.711)</td><td data-numeric="true">64 kbps</td><td>Fallback codec</td></tr><tr><td>PCMA (G.711)</td><td data-numeric="true">64 kbps</td><td>Fallback codec</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Recommended minimum bandwidth per call:</strong></span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Audio only:</strong> 100 kbps (including overhead)</li>
<li><strong>With video:</strong> 500-2000 kbps depending on resolution</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p"><strong>RTT requirements:</strong></span>

{\n  background-color: #00CFEB;\n  color: #012A33;\n  padding: 32px;\n  border-radius: 12px;\n  margin: 24px 0;\n}\n\n.ultra-hero h2 {\n  color: #012A33;\n  margin-top: 0;\n  font-size: 24px;\n}\n\n.ultra-hero p {\n  font-size: 16px;\n  line-height: 1.6;\n  color: #012A33;\n}\n\n.ultra-hero a {\n  color: #012A33;\n  text-decoration: underline;\n  font-weight: 600;\n}\n\n/* Beta tag pill in sidebar - increase font size */\n[class*=\"sidebar\"] [class*=\"tag\"],\n[class*=\"sidebar\"] [class*=\"badge\"],\nnav [class*=\"tag\"],\nnav [class*=\"badge\"] {\n  font-size: 0.8rem !important;\n}\n\n/* Keep the OneTrust cookie preferences control clear of the left sidebar. */\n#ot-sdk-btn-floating {\n  left: auto !important;\n  right: 10px !important;\n}\n\n/* Keep the preview-only widget and cookie preferences control from colliding. */\nbody:has([aria-label=\"Preview Widget\"]) #ot-sdk-btn-floating {\n  bottom: 88px !important;\n}\n\n/*\n * Fundamentals is a routed collection, not a product tab. Mintlify reveals a\n * hidden tab when one of its pages is active, so keep its unique tab control\n * out of the product navigation in that state as well.\n */\n#navbar .nav-tabs-item:has(\u003e div \u003e svg[style*=\"/book-open.svg\"]) {\n  display: none;\n}\n\n/* Mini-site tab rows show only the active tab, from first paint: the server\n   marks it with aria-current=\"location\", so pruning needs no JS.\n   home-directory.js only re-syncs inline state across SPA navigations. */\n.nav-tabs a.nav-tabs-item:not([aria-current=\"location\"]) { display: none; }\n\n/* Single-tab mini-sites (Other APIs, Agent SDK, Networking) get a tab row\n   whose one tab just repeats the product name — a redundant \"⋯ Other APIs\"\n   band under the navbar. Hide the row's container (the row itself sits in\n   a fixed-height wrapper, so hiding .nav-tabs alone would leave an empty\n   band) when the row holds exactly one authored tab. Tab anchors are the\n   row's only children, so :first-child:last-child counts authored tabs;\n   multi-tab rows keep their pruned active-tab title. */\n#navbar div:has(\u003e div \u003e .nav-tabs \u003e a.nav-tabs-item:first-child:last-child) {\n  display: none;\n}\n\n/* Desktop homepage uses the directory; section pages use native sidebars. */\n@media (width \u003e= 64rem) {\n  .txd-side { display: block; }\n  button[data-component-part=\"products-selector-trigger\"],\n  button.tx-product-switcher { display: none !important; }\n}\n/* Match desktop's absent stock switcher without hiding the native Home link. */\n#mobile-nav [data-component-part=\"products-selector-trigger\"],\n#mobile-nav #navigation-items \u003e div:has(\u003e div \u003e button[aria-haspopup=\"menu\"]),\n#mobile-nav.tx-has-directory #navigation-items {\n  display: none !important;\n}\n#mobile-nav .tx-mobile-directory { padding: 0; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; }\n#mobile-nav .tx-mobile-directory a,\n#mobile-nav .tx-mobile-directory .tx-dir-suite-head {\n  min-height: 2.75rem;\n}\n#mobile-nav .tx-mobile-directory :is(a, .tx-dir-suite-head):focus-visible {\n  outline: 2px solid currentColor; outline-offset: -2px;\n}\n\n/* --- Home directory ------------------------------------------------------\n   Server-rendered into docs/overview.mdx (mode: custom) between the tx-tree\n   markers by scripts/generate-home-directory.js. The links are present in the\n   initial HTML; home-directory.js adds progressive disclosure on desktop.\n   The .txd-* classes lay out the custom page. */\n/* Geometry mirrors the native mini-site layout so the sidebar doesn't\n   jump when crossing home \u003c-\u003e product pages: native #navigation-items sits\n   flush left at 18rem wide and the content column starts 476px in\n   (measured at 1600px). */\n.txd-shell {\n  display: flex; gap: 0; max-width: none;\n  margin: 0; padding: 3rem 2rem 6rem 0;\n}\n.txd-side { width: 18rem; flex-shrink: 0; }\n.txd-main { flex: 1; min-width: 0; max-width: 56rem; margin-left: 11.75rem; }\n@media (max-width: 80rem) {\n  .txd-main { margin-left: 4rem; }\n}\n@media (width \u003c 64rem) {\n  .txd-shell { display: block; padding: 2rem 1.5rem 5rem; }\n  .txd-side { display: none; }\n  .txd-main { margin-left: 0; }\n}\n\n/* CF-style home directory (shared by the static tree) */\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) { margin: 0 0 0.5rem; padding: 0 0.5rem 0 1rem; }\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-label {\n  font-size: 0.72rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.06em;\n  text-transform: uppercase; opacity: 0.55; margin: 0 0 0.4rem;\n}\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-label-gap { margin-top: 1.5rem; }\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-link,\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) summary {\n  display: flex; align-items: center; cursor: pointer;\n  font-size: 0.875rem; line-height: 1.25rem; padding: 0.35rem 0.5rem;\n  border-radius: 0.375rem; color: rgb(55 65 81); text-decoration: none;\n}\nhtml.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-link,\nhtml.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) summary { color: rgb(156 163 175); }\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-link:hover,\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) summary:hover { color: rgb(17 24 39); background: rgb(0 0 0 / 0.04); }\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-link[aria-current=\"page\"] {\n  color: #00b688; background: transparent; box-shadow: none; font-weight: 400;\n  text-shadow: -0.2px 0 0 currentColor, 0.2px 0 0 currentColor;\n}\nhtml.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-link:hover,\nhtml.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) summary:hover { color: rgb(229 231 235); background: rgb(255 255 255 / 0.06); }\nhtml.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-link[aria-current=\"page\"] {\n  color: #5eead4; background: transparent; box-shadow: none;\n}\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite { margin-bottom: 0.15rem; }\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite-head {\n  display: flex; align-items: center; cursor: pointer; user-select: none;\n  font-size: 0.875rem; line-height: 1.25rem; font-weight: 500;\n  padding: 0.35rem 0.5rem; border-radius: 0.375rem; color: rgb(55 65 81);\n}\nhtml.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite-head { color: rgb(156 163 175); }\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite-head:hover { color: rgb(17 24 39); background: rgb(0 0 0 / 0.04); }\nhtml.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite-head:hover { color: rgb(229 231 235); background: rgb(255 255 255 / 0.06); }\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite-head::after {\n  content: ''; width: 0.42rem; height: 0.42rem; margin-left: auto;\n  border-right: 1.5px solid currentColor; border-bottom: 1.5px solid currentColor;\n  transform: rotate(-45deg); transition: transform 0.15s; flex-shrink: 0; opacity: 0.7;\n}\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite.open \u003e .tx-dir-suite-head::after { transform: rotate(45deg); }\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite.open \u003e .tx-dir-suite-head {\n  color: rgb(17 24 39); background: rgb(0 0 0 / 0.04); font-weight: 600;\n}\nhtml.dark :is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite.open \u003e .tx-dir-suite-head {\n  color: rgb(229 231 235); background: rgb(255 255 255 / 0.06);\n}\n/* Collapsed by default at first paint; home-directory.js toggles .open.\n   Links stay in the served HTML either way, so crawlers see everything. */\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite \u003e ul { display: none; }\n:is(#tx-directory, .tx-mobile-directory) .tx-dir-suite.open \u003e ul { display: block; line-height: 1.55; font-weight: 500; margin: 0.9rem 0 1.3rem; color: rgb(31 41 55); }\nhtml.dark .txd-main .txd-lead { color: rgb(209 213 219); }\n.txd-main .txd-h2 { font-family: var(--font-family-headings-custom, \"PP Formula\"), -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", system-ui, sans-serif; }\n\n/* Homepage content uses Mintlify's native Card, Callout, and CodeGroup\n   components.
