a:focus-visible {\n  outline: 2px solid #00b688;\n  outline-offset: 3px;\n}\n@media (max-width: 48rem) {\n  .txd-main .txd-h1 { font-size: 1.9rem; }\n}\n@media (min-width: 64.01rem) and (max-width: 90rem) {\n  .txd-main { margin-right: 4rem; }\n}\n\n/* Agent SDK ownership comparison on the overview page. */\n.agent-sdk-ownership-matrix {\n  --matrix-border: #dbe4e1;\n  --matrix-panel: #ffffff;\n  --matrix-soft: #f7faf9;\n  --matrix-text: #17201e;\n  --matrix-muted: #64748b;\n  --matrix-you-bg: #eef2f6;\n  --matrix-you-text: #334155;\n  --matrix-telnyx-bg: #d8faf0;\n  --matrix-telnyx-text: #08785d;\n  margin: 1.5rem 0 2rem;\n}\n\n.dark .agent-sdk-ownership-matrix {\n  --matrix-border: #29343a;\n  --matrix-panel: #11171b;\n  --matrix-soft: #0e1418;\n  --matrix-text: #e7efec;\n  --matrix-muted: #9ca3af;\n  --matrix-you-bg: #26313a;\n  --matrix-you-text: #dbe4e8;\n  --matrix-telnyx-bg: #103d34;\n  --matrix-telnyx-text: #5eead4;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-ownership-scroll {\n  overflow-x: auto;\n  padding: 1px 1px 8px;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-ownership-grid {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: minmax(126px, .82fr) repeat(3, minmax(156px, 1fr));\n  min-width: 640px;\n  overflow: hidden;\n  color: var(--matrix-text);\n  background: var(--matrix-panel);\n  border: 1px solid var(--matrix-border);\n  border-radius: 16px;\n  box-shadow: 0 12px 35px rgb(15 23 42 / 7%);\n}\n\n.dark .agent-sdk-ownership-grid {\n  box-shadow: 0 16px 40px rgb(0 0 0 / 20%);\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-row {\n  display: contents;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-row \u003e * {\n  border-right: 1px solid var(--matrix-border);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--matrix-border);\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-row \u003e *:last-child {\n  border-right: 0;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-row-last \u003e * {\n  border-bottom: 0;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-corner,\n.agent-sdk-matrix-way {\n  min-height: 118px;\n  padding: 20px 18px 17px;\n  background: var(--matrix-soft);\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-corner {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: flex-end;\n  color: var(--matrix-muted);\n  font-size: .74rem;\n  font-weight: 800;\n  letter-spacing: .02em;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-way {\n  position: relative;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 5px;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-way::before {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 0;\n  right: 0;\n  left: 0;\n  height: 4px;\n  content: \"\";\n  background: var(--way-color);\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-way.way-one { --way-color: #f28c3a; }\n.agent-sdk-matrix-way.way-two { --way-color: #18b99b; }\n.agent-sdk-matrix-way.way-three { --way-color: #a875f5; }\n\n.agent-sdk-way-number {\n  color: var(--way-color);\n  font-size: .72rem;\n  font-weight: 850;\n  letter-spacing: .03em;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-way-title {\n  font-family: \"PP Formula\", -apple-system, \"system-ui\", \"Segoe UI\", sans-serif;\n  color: var(--matrix-text);\n  font-size: .94rem;\n  font-weight: 800;\n  line-height: 1.25;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-way-caption {\n  color: var(--matrix-muted);\n  font-size: .72rem;\n  font-weight: 650;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-layer,\n.agent-sdk-matrix-owner {\n  min-height: 64px;\n  padding: 14px 18px;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-layer {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  font-family: \"PP Formula\", -apple-system, \"system-ui\", \"Segoe UI\", sans-serif;\n  color: var(--matrix-text);\n  background: var(--matrix-soft);\n  font-size: .88rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-owner {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  background: var(--matrix-panel);\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-owner span {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 7px;\n  padding: 6px 10px;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  font-size: .72rem;\n  font-weight: 800;\n  letter-spacing: .02em;\n  white-space: nowrap;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-owner span::before {\n  width: 6px;\n  height: 6px;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  content: \"\";\n  background: currentColor;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-owner .owner-you {\n  color: var(--matrix-you-text);\n  background: var(--matrix-you-bg);\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-matrix-owner .owner-telnyx {\n  color: var(--matrix-telnyx-text);\n  background: var(--matrix-telnyx-bg);\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-shared-foundation {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: minmax(126px, .82fr) 1fr auto;\n  min-width: 640px;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 18px;\n  margin-top: 10px;\n  padding: 14px 18px;\n  color: #0b5e4d;\n  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #dff8f0, #eefbf7);\n  border: 1px solid #8ed8c4;\n  border-radius: 13px;\n}\n\n.dark .agent-sdk-shared-foundation {\n  color: #99f6e4;\n  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #10352f, #102521);\n  border-color: #247865;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-shared-foundation span {\n  font-size: .7rem;\n  font-weight: 800;\n  letter-spacing: .02em;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-shared-foundation span:last-child {\n  color: var(--matrix-muted);\n  font-size: .64rem;\n}\n\n.agent-sdk-foundation-name {\n  font-family: \"PP Formula\", -apple-system, \"system-ui\", \"Segoe UI\", sans-serif;\n  color: inherit;\n  font-size: .94rem;\n  font-weight: 800;\n  letter-spacing: 0;\n  text-transform: none;\n}\n\n/* Search-first directory on generated SDK resource-map pages. */\n.rm-explorer {\n  --rm-border: rgb(15 23 42 / 14%);\n  --rm-muted: #5f6b68;\n  --rm-soft: #f6f8f7;\n}\n\n.dark .rm-explorer {\n  --rm-border: rgb(255 255 255 / 14%);\n  --rm-muted: #a2aaa8;\n  --rm-soft: #151a19;\n}\n\n#resource-map-toolbar {\n  position: sticky;\n  top: 5rem;\n  z-index: 20;\n  margin: 1.25rem 0 1.75rem;\n  padding: .75rem 0;\n  background: var(--rm-surface, #fff);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rm-border);\n}\n\n.dark #resource-map-toolbar {\n  background: #0b0e0d;\n}\n\n.rm-controls {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: minmax(15rem, 1fr) minmax(10rem, auto) auto;\n  gap: .625rem;\n  align-items: center;\n}\n\n.rm-search {\n  position: relative;\n  min-width: 0;\n}\n\n.rm-search svg {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 50%;\n  left: .75rem;\n  width: 1rem;\n  height: 1rem;\n  color: var(--rm-muted);\n  fill: none;\n  stroke: currentColor;\n  stroke-width: 1.8;\n  transform: translateY(-50%);\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.rm-search input,\n.rm-area-select,\n.rm-collapse,\n.rm-clear,\n.rm-empty button {\n  min-height: 2.5rem;\n  color: inherit;\n  background: transparent;\n  border: 1px solid var(--rm-border);\n  border-radius: .5rem;\n  font: inherit;\n  font-size: .875rem;\n}\n\n.rm-search input {\n  width: 100%;\n  padding: .58rem 2.6rem .58rem 2.35rem;\n}\n\n.rm-search input::placeholder {\n  color: var(--rm-muted);\n  opacity: 1;\n}\n\n.rm-shortcut {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 50%;\n  right: .65rem;\n  display: grid;\n  width: 1.5rem;\n  height: 1.5rem;\n  place-items: center;\n  color: var(--rm-muted);\n  background: var(--rm-soft);\n  border: 1px solid var(--rm-border);\n  border-radius: .35rem;\n  font: inherit;\n  font-size: .72rem;\n  transform: translateY(-50%);\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.rm-area-wrap {\n  position: relative;\n  max-width: 13rem;\n}\n\n.rm-area-select {\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: 13rem;\n  padding: .55rem 2.25rem .55rem .75rem;\n  appearance: none;\n  cursor: pointer;\n}\n\n.rm-area-caret {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 50%;\n  right: .85rem;\n  width: .45rem;\n  height: .45rem;\n  border-right: 1.5px solid currentColor;\n  border-bottom: 1.5px solid currentColor;\n  transform: translateY(-70%) rotate(45deg);\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.rm-collapse,\n.rm-clear,\n.rm-empty button {\n  padding: .5rem .75rem;\n  font-weight: 650;\n  cursor: pointer;\n}\n\n.rm-search input:focus-visible,\n.rm-area-select:focus-visible,\n.rm-collapse:focus-visible,\n.rm-clear:focus-visible,\n.rm-empty button:focus-visible {\n  outline: 2px solid #00b688;\n  outline-offset: 2px;\n  border-color: #00b688;\n  box-shadow: none !important;\n}\n\n.rm-collapse:hover,\n.rm-clear:hover,\n.rm-empty button:hover {\n  color: #08785d;\n  background: var(--rm-soft);\n}\n\n.dark .rm-collapse:hover,\n.dark .rm-clear:hover,\n.dark .rm-empty button:hover {\n  color: #5eead4;\n}\n\n.rm-meta {\n  display: flex;\n  min-height: 1.65rem;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: .75rem;\n  padding: .45rem .15rem 0;\n}\n\n.rm-status {\n  color: var(--rm-muted);\n  font-size: .78rem;\n  line-height: 1.35;\n}\n\n.rm-clear {\n  min-height: 1.8rem;\n  padding: .2rem .55rem;\n  border-color: transparent;\n  font-size: .78rem;\n}\n\n.rm-empty {\n  display: grid;\n  justify-items: start;\n  gap: .35rem;\n  margin: -.5rem 0 1.75rem;\n  padding: 1.1rem 1.2rem;\n  color: var(--rm-muted);\n  background: var(--rm-soft);\n  border: 1px solid var(--rm-border);\n  border-radius: .75rem;\n  font-size: .875rem;\n}\n\n.rm-empty strong {\n  color: inherit;\n  font-size: .95rem;\n}\n\n.rm-empty button {\n  min-height: 2.2rem;\n  margin-top: .35rem;\n}\n\n.rm-resource-list {\n  display: block !important;\n  margin: 0 0 .75rem !important;\n  border-color: var(--rm-border) !important;\n  border-top: 0 !important;\n  border-radius: 0 0 .75rem .75rem !important;\n}\n\n.rm-resource-list \u003e details.accordion {\n  margin: 0 !important;\n  border: 0 !important;\n  border-radius: 0 !important;\n}\n\n.rm-resource-list \u003e details.accordion + details.accordion {\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--rm-border) !important;\n}\n\n.rm-resource-list \u003e details.accordion \u003e summary {\n  min-height: 3rem;\n  padding: .7rem .9rem !important;\n  border-radius: 0 !important;\n}\n\n.rm-resource-list \u003e details.accordion:first-child \u003e summary resolve.\n *\n * Usage:\n *   node scripts/docs/check-llms-links.js                # check both files\n *   node scripts/docs/check-llms-links.js --internal     # only developers.telnyx.com links\n *   node scripts/docs/check-llms-links.js --fail-on-broken  # exit 1 if any broken\n *\n * Why: the AFDocs \"LLMS Full Links Resolve\" check fails when llms-full.txt\n * contains links that 404. Run this in CI to catch regressions before they\n * ship to developers.telnyx.com.\n */\n\nconst fs = require(\"fs\");\nconst path = require(\"path\");\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, \"../..\");\n\n// Resolve the file list at runtime so the per-section indexes and\n// api-reference shards produced by the generator into llms-out/ are\n// validated automatically (run `npm run generate:llms` first).\nfunction discoverFiles() {\n  const candidates = [\"llms.txt\", \"llms-full.txt\"];\n  const llmsOut = path.join(REPO_ROOT, \"llms-out\");\n  if (fs.existsSync(llmsOut)) {\n    for (const name of fs.readdirSync(llmsOut)) {\n      if (/\\.txt$/.test(name)) candidates.push(path.join(\"llms-out\", name));\n    }\n  }\n  return candidates;\n}\nconst FILES = discoverFiles();\nconst INTERNAL_HOST = \"developers.telnyx.com\";\nconst CONCURRENCY = 8;\nconst TIMEOUT_MS = 15000;\nconst RETRY_ATTEMPTS = 2;\n\n// 401/403/429 are commonly returned by API endpoints, bot-detection, and\n// rate limiters — they prove the host is reachable, not that the URL is\n// broken. AFDocs only flags genuinely broken links, so we mirror that.\nconst ACCEPTABLE_STATUSES = new Set([200, 201, 202, 204, 301, 302, 303, 307, 308, 401, 403, 405, 429]);\n\nconst args = new Set(process.argv.slice(2));\nconst internalOnly = args.has(\"--internal\");\nconst failOnBroken = args.has(\"--fail-on-broken\");\n\nfunction extractLinks(text) {\n  const out = new Set();\n  const re = /\\]\\((https?:\\/\\/[^)\\s]+)\\)/g;\n  let m;\n  while ((m = re.exec(text)) !== null) out.add(m[1]);\n  return [...out];\n}\n\n// Some sites (api.telnyx.com, gov.au pages with bot protection) reject HEAD\n// with 404/405 even though GET works. Fall back to GET on any 4xx HEAD\n// before declaring the link broken. Connection errors and timeouts retry\n// up to RETRY_ATTEMPTS times to avoid flagging transient flakes.\nasync function attemptOnce(url) {\n  const controller = new AbortController();\n  const timer = setTimeout(() =\u003e controller.abort(), TIMEOUT_MS);\n  const ua =\n    \"Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; LlmsLinkCheck/1.0; +https://developers.telnyx.com)\";\n  try {\n    let res = await fetch(url, {\n      method: \"HEAD\",\n      redirect: \"follow\",\n      signal: controller.signal,\n      headers: { \"User-Agent\": ua },\n    });\n    if (res.status \u003e= 400 \u0026\u0026 res.status !== 401 \u0026\u0026 res.status !== 403 \u0026\u0026 res.status !== 429) {\n      res = await fetch(url, {\n        method: \"GET\",\n        redirect: \"follow\",\n        signal: controller.signal,\n        headers: { \"User-Agent\": ua },\n      });\n    }\n    return res.status;\n  } catch (_) {\n    return 0;\n  } finally {\n    clearTimeout(timer);\n  }\n}\n\nasync function checkOne(url) {\n  let runPool(items, fn) {\n  const results = [];\n  let i = 0;\n  async function worker() {\n    while (i \u003c items.length) {\n      const idx = i++;\n      results[idx] = await fn(items[idx]);\n    }\n /s3\\.[a-z0-9-]+\\.amazonaws\\.com/i,\n  /https:\\/\\/developers\\.telnyx\\.com\\/docs\\/development\\/(?:llms\\/[^)\\s]+-llms-(?:full-)?txt|llms-txt(?:-full)?)\\.md/i,\n];\nconst NATIVE_ROUTE =\n  /https:\\/\\/developers\\.telnyx\\.com\\/(docs\\/development\\/llms[^)\\s.]+)/g;\n\nfunction read(relativePath) {\n  return fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, relativePath), \"utf8\");\n}\n\nfunction const jsObjectString = toJSObjectString(coverageData);\n  const indentedObject = jsObjectString.split('\\n').map((line, i) =\u003e i === 0 ? line : '  ' + line).join('\\n');\n  const newDataString = status\"]);\n    if (httpColumn \u003e= 0 \u0026\u0026 httpColumn !== identity.index \u0026\u0026 row[httpColumn]) {\n      entry.http_status = cleanCell(row[httpColumn]);\n    } else if cleanCell(row[statusColumn]);\n      }\n    }\n    entries.push(entry);\n  }\n  return entries;\n}\n\nfunction parseSource(source, root) {\n  const absolute = path.join(root, source.path);\n  if (!fs.existsSync(absolute)) throw new Error(`Registered source is missing: ${source.path}`);\n  let tables = parseTables(fs.readFileSync(absolute, \"utf8\"));\n  if (Array.isArray(source.sections) \u0026\u0026 source.sections.length \u003e 0) {\n    const requested = new Set(source.sections);\n    tables = tables.filter((table) =\u003e requested.has(table.heading));\n    const found = new Set(tables.map((table) =\u003e table.heading));\n    const missing = source.sections.filter((section) =\u003e !found.has(section));\n    if (missing.length \u003e 0) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `Registered source sections are missing from ${source.path}: ${missing.join(\", \")}`\n      );\n    }\n  }\n  const entries = tables.flatMap((table, index) =\u003e tableEntries(table, source, index));\n  if (entries.length === 0) {\n    throw new Error(`No error/reference rows parsed from registered source: ${source.path}`);\n  }\n  return entries;\n}\n\nfunction candidatePath(relativePath) {\n  const value = relativePath.toLowerCase();\n  return (\n    !value.startsWith(\"docs/development/llms\") \u0026\u0026\n    [\"/error\", \"/errors\", \"troubleshooting\", \"response-codes\"].some((marker) =\u003e\n      value.includes(marker)\n    )\n  );\n}\n\nconst ERROR_REFERENCE_HEADING =\n  /\\b(error|errors|failure|failures|failed|response codes?|close codes?|troubleshoot(?:ing)?)\\b/i;\n\nfunction candidateTable(table, relativePath) {\n  if (!identityColumn(table.headers)) return false;\n  return (\n    candidatePath(relativePath) ||\n    ERROR_REFERENCE_HEADING.test(table.heading || \"\") ||\n    table.headers.some((header) =\u003e [\"error code\", \"close code\"].includes(header))\n  );\n}\n\nfunction walkMdx(directory, root, output = []) {\n  if (!fs.existsSync(directory)) return output;\n  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(directory, { withFileTypes: true })) {\n    const absolute = path.join(directory, entry.name);\n    if (entry.isDirectory()) walkMdx(absolute, root, output);\n    else if (entry.isFile() \u0026\u0026 entry.name.endsWith(\".mdx\")) {\n      output.push(path.relative(root, absolute).split(path.sep).join(\"/\"));\n    }\n  }\n  return output;\n}\n\nfunction discoverCandidateSources(root) {\n  const candidates = [];\n  for (const relative of walkMdx(path.join(root, \"docs\"), root)) {\n    if (relative.toLowerCase().startsWith(\"docs/development/llms\")) continue;\n    const mdx = fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, relative), \"utf8\");\n    if (parseTables(mdx).some((table) =\u003e candidateTable(table, relative))) {\n      candidates.push(relative);\n    }\n  }\n  return [...new Set(candidates)].sort();\n}\n\nfunction validateRegistry(registry, root) {\n  if (registry.schema_version !== 1 || !Array.isArray(registry.sources)) {\n    throw new Error(\"Error catalog registry must use schema_version 1 and define sources\");\n  }\n  const registered = new Set(registry.sources.map((source) =\u003e source.path));\n  const exclusions = new Map(\n    (registry.exclusions || []).map((item) =\u003e [item.path, item.reason])\n  );\n  for (const [excludedPath, reason] of exclusions) {\n    if (!reason) throw new Error(`Registry exclusion lacks a reason: ${excludedPath}`);\n  }\n  const unregistered = discoverCandidateSources(root).filter(\n    (candidate) =\u003e !registered.has(candidate) \u0026\u0026 !exclusions.has(candidate)\n  );\n  if (unregistered.length \u003e 0) {\n    throw new Error(`Unregistered parseable error/reference pages:\\n  ${unregistered.join(\"\\n  \")}`);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction generateCatalog(registry, root = ROOT) {\n  validateRegistry(registry, root);\n  const errors = registry.sources.flatMap((source) =\u003e parseSource(source, root));\n  errors.sort((left, right) =\u003e\n    [left.product, left.source, left.code || left.status, left.id]\n      .join(\"\\u0000\")\n      .localeCompare(\n        [right.product, right.source, right.code || right.status, right.id].join(\"\\u0000\"),\n        \"en\"\n      )\n  );\n\n  const productCounts = new Map();\n  const sourceCounts = new Map();\n  const codeScopes = new Map();\n  for (const error of errors) {\n    productCounts.set(error.product, (productCounts.get(error.product) || 0) + 1);\n    sourceCounts.set(error.source, (sourceCounts.get(error.source) || 0) + 1);\n    const code = error.code || error.status;\n    const scope = `${error.product} | ${error.source}`;\n    if (!codeScopes.has(code)) codeScopes.set(code, []);\n    codeScopes.get(code).push(scope);\n  }\n\n  return {\n    schema_version: 1,\n    title: \"Telnyx API and product error catalog\",\n    description:\n      \"Unified machine-readable catalog generated from maintained Telnyx error and response-code references. Fields are preserved only when authored by the source page.\",\n    registry: \"data/error-catalog-sources.json\",\n    count: errors.length,\n    product_count: productCounts.size,\n    source_count: sourceCounts.size,\n    products: [...productCounts].map(([product, count]) =\u003e ({ product, count })),\n    sources: registry.sources.map((source) =\u003e ({\n      ...source,\n      count: sourceCounts.get(source.path) || 0,\n      source_url: sourceUrl(source.path),\n    })),\n    duplicate_codes: [...codeScopes]\n      .filter(([, scopes]) =\u003e scopes.length \u003e 1)\n      .map(([code, scopes]) =\u003e ({\n        code,\n        occurrences: scopes.length,\n        scopes: [...new Set(scopes)].sort(),\n      })),\n    errors,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction renderCatalog(registry, root = ROOT) {\n  return `${JSON.stringify(generateCatalog(registry, root), null, 2)}\\n`;\n}\n\nfunction main() {\n  const registry = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(REGISTRY_PATH, \"utf8\"));\n  let rendered;\n  try {\n    rendered = renderCatalog(registry, ROOT);\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(error.message);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  const catalog = JSON.parse(rendered);\n  if (process.argv.includes(\"--check\")) {\n    const existing = fs.existsSync(OUTPUT_PATH) ? fs.readFileSync(OUTPUT_PATH, \"utf8\") : \"\";\n    if (existing !== rendered) {\n      console.error(\"data/api-errors.json is stale — run: npm run generate:error-catalog\");\n      process.exit(1);\n    }\n    console.log(\n      `OK: ${catalog.count} entries across ${catalog.product_count} products and ${catalog.source_count} sources`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(OUTPUT_PATH), { recursive: true });\n  fs.writeFileSync(OUTPUT_PATH, rendered);\n  console.log(`Wrote ${catalog.count} entries to data/api-errors.json`);\n}\n\nmodule.exports = {\n  discoverCandidateSources,\n  generateCatalog,\n  parseTables,\n  renderCatalog,\n  splitTableRow,\n};\n\nif (require.main === module) main();\n"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"6c:T124ac,"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"#!/usr/bin/env node\n\n/**\n * Generate llms.txt artifacts for the Telnyx developer docs.\n *\n * Usage: node scripts/docs/generate-llms-txt.js\n *\n * Reads docs.json from the repo root, walks the navigation tree,\n * and produces:\n *\n * Committed to the repo (served by Mintlify, which only serves these\n * exact filenames — any other root/public .txt 404s):\n *   llms.txt             – root index (\u003c50K) linking every per-section and\n *                          per-subsection file; overrides Mintlify's auto\n *                          llms.txt\n *   llms-full.txt        – \u003c200K map: auth + quickstarts + links to the\n *                          per-area full-content files (overrides auto version)\n *\n * Also committed — regenerated on every run as minimal Mintlify page\n * registrations whose routes redirect to the raw contextual assets:\n *   docs/development/llms/\u003ctab\u003e-llms-txt/index.mdx\n *   docs/development/llms/\u003ctab\u003e-llms-full-txt/index.mdx\n *   docs/development/llms/\u003ctab\u003e-\u003csub\u003e-llms-txt/index.mdx\n *   docs/development/llms/\u003ctab\u003e-\u003csub\u003e-llms-full-txt/index.mdx\n *   docs/development/llms/code-examples/index.mdx – hidden page carrying the\n *                          code-examples snippet, linked from llms.txt\n *   docs.json            – a \"For AI Agents\" group inside every subsection\n *                          pointing at the two landing pages (idempotent)\n *\n * Temporary build artifacts are written to llms-out/ and copied into the\n * contextual raw assets under public/llms/. llms-out/ itself remains gitignored:\n *   llms-out/\u003ctab\u003e.txt            – per-section index (pages + endpoints)\n *   llms-out/\u003ctab\u003e-full.txt       – per-section full page content\n *   llms-out/\u003ctab\u003e/\u003csub\u003e.txt      – per-subsection index\n *   llms-out/\u003ctab\u003e/\u003csub\u003e-full.txt – per-subsection full page content\n *\n * All links inside llms-out/ files are absolute so copied static assets do\n * not depend on their eventual directory depth.\n *\n * Follows the llms.txt convention: https://llmstxt.org/\n */\n\nconst fs = require(\"fs\");\nconst path = require(\"path\");\nconst yaml = require(\"js-yaml\");\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, \"../..\");\nconst DOCS_JSON_PATH = path.join(REPO_ROOT, \"docs.json\");\nconst PUBLIC_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, \"public\");\nconst LLMS_OUT_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, \"llms-out\");\nconst RAW_LLMS_DIR = path.join(PUBLIC_DIR, \"llms\");\n// Generated sidebar landing pages live here (wiped + rewritten every run).\nconst LANDING_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, \"docs\", \"development\", \"llms\");\nconst BASE_URL = \"https://developers.telnyx.com\";\nconst CONSOLIDATED_OPENAPI_SPEC_URL =\n  \"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/team-telnyx/openapi/master/openapi/spec3.json\";\n\n\n// Slugify a title/tag the same way Mintlify renders OpenAPI operation routes:\n// apostrophes, slashes, and parentheses are stripped (not hyphenated);\n// underscores are preserved as-is; everything else collapses to single hyphens.\nfunction slugifyOpenApi(s) {\n  return (s || \"\")\n    .toLowerCase()\n    .replace(/['/()]/g, \"\")\n    .replace(/[^a-z0-9_]+/g, \"-\")\n    .replace(/^-|-$/g, \"\");\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Tabs: slugs and descriptions for the per-section files\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst TAB_SLUGS = {\n  \"AI\": \"ai\",\n  \"Agent SDK\": \"ai-agent-sdk-beta\",\n  \"Agent Tools\": \"agent-tools\",\n  \"AI\": \"ai\",\n  \"Compute\": \"compute\",\n  \"Storage\": \"storage\",\n  \"Calling\": \"calling\",\n  \"Voice\": \"voice\",\n  \"Messaging\": \"messaging\",\n  \"Numbers\": \"numbers\",\n  \"Wireless\": \"wireless\",\n  \"Fundamentals\": \"fundamentals\",\n};\n\n// Overview is the product landscape landing page, not a product corpus. Every\n// other published tab must have an explicit slug so navigation changes fail\n// closed instead of silently disappearing from the machine-readable indexes.\nconst LLMS_EXCLUDED_TABS = new Set([\"Overview\"]);\n\nfunction deriveTabOrder(docsJson) {\n  const tabs = navigationTabs(docsJson);\n  const order = [];\n  for (const tab of tabs) {\n    const tabName = tab.tab;\n    if (!tabName || LLMS_EXCLUDED_TABS.has(tabName)) continue;\n    if (!TAB_SLUGS[tabName]) {\n      throw new Error(`Missing LLMS slug for navigation tab \"${tabName}\"`);\n    }\n    order.push(tabName);\n  }\n  return order;\n}\n\nconst TAB_DESCRIPTIONS = {\n  \"AI\": \"Inference, embedding, AI assistants, missions, and LiveKit integrations\",\n  \"Agent SDK\":\n    \"Build and run stateful AI agents with scheduling, message history, SQL, and WebSockets\",\n  \"Agent Tools\":\n    \"Hosted tools for AI agents: web search, deep research, AI Search, memory, browser, payment, and meeting\",\n  \"AI\": \"Inference, embedding, AI assistants, missions, and LiveKit integrations\",\n  \"Calling\":\n    \"TeXML, SIP trunking, UAC connections, WebRTC, and trusted calling\",\n  \"Voice\": \"Voice API, speech-to-text, text-to-speech, and voice design\",\n  \"Messaging\":\n    \"SMS, MMS, RCS, WhatsApp, short codes, 10DLC compliance, and messaging profiles\",\n  \"Numbers\":\n    \"Phone number search and ordering, porting, regulatory requirements, and identity\",\n  \"Compute\": \"Edge compute functions, runtime bindings, stateful actors, and private networking\",\n  \"Storage\": \"S3-compatible cloud storage with buckets, SDKs, and migration from AWS\",\n  \"Wireless\": \"SIMs, eSIMs, IoT data plans, VoLTE, and wireless messaging\",\n  \"Fundamentals\":\n    \"Account setup, authentication, SDKs, developer tools, and supporting platform APIs\",\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Navigation shape adapter — legacy tabs view over the products division\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// docs.json uses navigation.products (Cloudflare-style suites: a directory\n// Home plus one mini-site per suite). This adapter presents the products\n// division as main's tab families so every walker below works on either\n// shape, and every published llms slug in TAB_SLUGS stays stable:\n//\n//   product \"Home\" → tab \"Fundamentals\" (account setup, auth, SDKs, CLI,\n//                    developer tools, and the supporting/other platform APIs)\n//   \"Networking\"   → family \"Compute\" (split out of Compute in the nav;\n//                    compute-network-* routes never move)\n//   every other suite → the same-named family\n//\n// A suite missing from TAB_SLUGS after this mapping makes generation fail\n// closed in deriveTabOrder — add the family there or map the suite here.\nconst PRODUCT_TO_TAB = {\n  \"Other APIs\": \"Fundamentals\",\n  \"Home\": \"Fundamentals\",\n  \"Networking\": \"Compute\",\n};\n\nconst HOME_PRODUCT = \"Home\";\n\nfunction productFamilyForNestedTab(productName, nestedTabName) {\n  return PRODUCT_TO_TAB[productName] || productName;\n}\n\n// Menu-item view over a nested product tab. Only the keys the walkers read\n// (item/groups/pages) — copying `tab` or `icon` through would corrupt the\n// walkers' navigation context.\nfunction menuItemView(nestedTab) {\n  const view = { item: nestedTab.tab };\n  if (nestedTab.groups) view.groups = nestedTab.groups;\n  if (nestedTab.pages) view.pages = nestedTab.pages;\n  return view;\n}\n\n// Read-only legacy view of the navigation. Group and page objects are shared\n// with docs.json, not cloned — walkers must not mutate them (the sidebar\n// updater walks the real shape itself for exactly that reason).\nfunction navigationTabs(docsJson) {\n  const navigation = docsJson.navigation || {};\n  if (navigation.tabs) return navigation.tabs;\n\n  const legacyTabs = new Map();\n  const legacyMenuTab = (name) =\u003e {\n    if (!legacyTabs.has(name)) legacyTabs.set(name, { tab: name, menu: [] });\n    return legacyTabs.get(name);\n  };\n\n  for (const product of navigation.products || []) {\n    if (product.product === HOME_PRODUCT) {\n      // Home's groups are the head of the flat Fundamentals family.\n      const family = PRODUCT_TO_TAB[HOME_PRODUCT];\n      const entry = legacyTabs.get(family) || { tab: family, groups: [] };\n      entry.groups = [...(product.groups || []), ...entry.groups];\n      legacyTabs.set(family, entry);\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (product.product === \"Other APIs\") {\n      // Renders as its own mini-site, but its corpus stays the Fundamentals\n      // family's \"Other APIs\" subsection — the same nested shape it had as\n   for (const nestedTab of product.tabs || []) {\n        entry.groups.push({ group: nestedTab.tab, pages: nestedTab.groups || [] });\n      }\n      legacyTabs.set(family, entry);\n      continue;\n    }\n    for (const nestedTab of product.tabs || []) {\n      const family = productFamilyForNestedTab(product.product, nestedTab.tab);\n      legacyMenuTab(family).menu.push(menuItemView(nestedTab));\n    }\n  }\n  return [...legacyTabs.values()];\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// (AGENTS.md, .cursorrules, .claude/, .windsurf/): https://github.com/team-telnyx/ai\n- Machine-readable capabilities: https://telnyx.com/ai/capabilities.json · Pricing: https://telnyx.com/pricing.md · Agent fast path: https://telnyx.com/agents/start\n- Differentiation vs alternatives: https://developers.telnyx.com/compare\n\n`;\n\nconst RELIABILITY_MD = `## Reliability \u0026 Status\n\n- Status https://status.telnyx.com/api/v2/summary.json · recent incident history at https://status.telnyx.com/api/v2/incidents.json · per-component status at https://status.telnyx.com/api/v2/components.json\n- Incident feeds: RSS at https://status.telnyx.com/history.rss · Atom at https://status.telnyx.com/history.atom\n- SLA, machine-readable: https://telnyx.com/ai/sla.json (99.99% uptime guarantee, WHY_TELNYX_MD = `## Why Telnyx (vs alternatives)\n\nUnlike reseller CPaaS platforms (e.g. Twilio or Vonage), Telnyx owns and operates its global carrier network and runs AI inference on its own GPU infrastructure — one platform and one bill for voice, messaging, numbers, wireless, and AI, with usage-based rates published openly at https://telnyx.com/pricing.md. See https://telnyx.com/why-telnyx, the comparison page at https://developers.telnyx.com/compare.md, and the Twilio migration guide in the Fundamentals section.\n\n`;\n\nconst AUTH_QUICKSTART_MD = `## Authentication\n\nEvery API request authenticates with a Bearer API key:\n\n\\`\\`\\`bash\ncurl https://api.telnyx.com/v2/available_phone_numbers \\\\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer $TELNYX_API_KEY\"\n\\`\\`\\`\n\nCreate a key in the Mission Control Portal (https://portal.telnyx.com) or programmatically via the agent signup flow (https://telnyx.com/agent-signup.md).\n\n## Quickstart examples\n\nSend an SMS:\n\n\\`\\`\\`bash\ncurl -X POST https://api.telnyx.com/v2/messages \\\\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer $TELNYX_API_KEY\" \\\\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" slugifyOpenApi(subName);\n}\n\nfunction llmsPageUrl(ref) {\n  return `${BASE_URL}/${ref.replace(/\\/index$/, \"\")}`;\n}\n\nfunction subIndexUrl(tabName, subName) {\n  return maps.\nfunction subKey(tabName, subName) {\n  return `${tabName} ${subName}`;\n}\n\n// Tab → ordered list of subsection names, straight from docs.json.\n// Derived dynamically so new menu items / groups are picked up with no\n// code change (a hardcoded map here went stale within weeks in May).\nfunction deriveSubsections(docsJson) {\n  const map = new Map();\n  for (const tab of    subs.push(n);\n      }\n    } else if (tab.groups) {\n      for (const g of tab.groups) {\n        const n = g.group;\n        if (!n || !n.trim() || SKIP_GROUPS.has(n)) continue;\n        subs.push(n);\n      }\n    }\n    map.set(tab.tab, subs);\n  }\n  return map;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction readDocsJson() {\n  const raw = fs.readFileSync(DOCS_JSON_PATH, \"utf8\");\n  return JSON.parse(raw);\n}\n\nfunction parseFrontmatter(content) {\n  const match = content.match(/^---\\r?\\n([\\s\\S]*?)\\r?\\n---/);\n  if (!match) return null;\n  const fm = {};\n  const lines = match[1].split(/\\r?\\n/);\n  for (const line of lines) {\n    const m = line.match(/^(\\w[\\w-]*):\\s*(.*)$/);\n    if (m) {\n      let val = m[2].trim();\n      if (\n        (val.startsWith('\"') \u0026\u0026 val.endsWith('\"')) ||\n        (val.startsWith(\"'\") \u0026\u0026 val.endsWith(\"'\"))\n      ) {\n        val = val.slice(1, -1);\n      }\n      fm[m[1]] = val;\n    }\n  }\n  return fm;\n}\n\nfunction stripFrontmatter(content) {\n  return content.replace(/^---\\r?\\n[\\s\\S]*?\\r?\\n---\\r?\\n?/, \"\");\n}\n\nfunction stripMdxComponents(content) {\n  const lines = content.split(\"\\n\");\n  const result = [];\n  let inCodeBlock = false;\n\n  for (const line of lines) {\n    if (line.trimStart().startsWith(\"```\")) {\n      inCodeBlock = !inCodeBlock;\n      result.push(line);\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (inCodeBlock) {\n      result.push(line);\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // Skip self-closing JSX tags\n    if (/^\u003c\\w+[^\u003e]*\\/\u003e\\s*$/.test(line.trim())) {\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // Skip opening JSX tags on their own line\n    if (/^\u003c\\w+[^\u003e]*\u003e\\s*$/.test(line.trim())) {\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // Skip closing JSX tags\n    if (/^\u003c\\/\\w+[^\u003e]*\u003e\\s*$/.test(line.trim())) {\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // Skip import statements\n    if (/^import\\s/.test(line.trim())) {\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // Skip export statements\n    if (/^export\\s/.test(line.trim())) {\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    result.push(line);\n  }\n\n  let output = result.join(\"\\n\");\n\n  // Code is content, not markup. Mask fenced blocks and inline spans before the\n  // JSX strippers run, or they eat angle-bracket placeholders out of commands and\n  // manifest keys — `env.\u003cNAME\u003e` becomes `env.` and\n  // `telnyx-edge auth api-key set \u003cYOUR_API_KEY\u003e` loses its argument.\n  const masked = [];\n  const mask = (match) =\u003e `\\u0000CODE${masked.push(match) - 1}\\u0000`;\n\n  // Fenced blocks first, so a comment or tag shown as an example survives.\n  output = output.replace(/```[\\s\\S]*?```/g, mask);\n\n  // MDX comments never render on the page, so they must not reach the text output.\n  output = output.replace(/\\{\\/\\*[\\s\\S]*?\\*\\/\\}/g, \"\");\n\n  // Inline code spans, which do not run across lines.\n  output = output.replace(/(`+)[^`\\n]+?\\1/g, mask);\n\n  // Remove self-closing JSX tags inline\n  output = output.replace(/\u003c\\w+[^\u003e]*\\/\u003e/g, \"\");\n\n  // Remove opening/closing tags but keep content between them\n  output = output.replace(/\u003c\\/?\\w+[^\u003e]*\u003e/g, \"\");\n\n  output = output.replace(/\\u0000CODE(\\d+)\\u0000/g, (_, i) =\u003e masked[Number(i)]);\n\n  // Collapse multiple blank lines to at most two\n  output = output.replace(/\\n{3,}/g, \"\\n\\n\");\n\n  return output.trim();\n}\n\nfunction pageRefToUrl(ref) {\n  let slug = ref;\n  if (slug.endsWith(\"/index\")) {\n    slug = slug.slice(0, -6);\n  }\n  return `${BASE_URL}/${slug}`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Append .md to same-origin URLs so agents get markdown instead of HTML.\n */\nfunction toMdUrl(url) {\n  if (url \u0026\u0026 url.startsWith(BASE_URL)) {\n    return url + \".md\";\n  }\n  return url;\n}\n\nfunction shouldSkip(ref) {\n  if (typeof ref !== \"string\") return false;\n  if (ref.startsWith(\"https://\")) return true;\n  if (/^(GET|POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE|OPTIONS|HEAD|TRACE)\\s\\//.test(ref)) return true;\n  return false;\n}\n\nfunction resolveMdxPath(ref) {\n  const p = path.join(REPO_ROOT, `${ref}.mdx`);\n  if (fs.existsSync(p)) return p;\n  return null;\n}\n\n// Truncate a frontmatter description for index bullets.\nfunction shortDescription(s, max = 200) {\n  if (!s) return \"\";\n  const oneLine = s.split(\"\\n\")[0].trim();\n  if (oneLine.length \u003c= max) return oneLine;\n  return oneLine.slice(0, max - 1).trimEnd() + \"…\";\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Navigation tree walker\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction walkNavigation(docsJson) {\n  const tabs = navigationTabs(docsJson);\n  const allPages = [];\n\n  for (const tab of tabs) {\n    const tabName = tab.tab;\n\n    if (tab.menu) {\n      for (const menuItem of tab.menu) {\n        const itemName = menuItem.item;\n        if (SKIP_GROUPS.has(itemName)) continue;\n\n        for (const group of menuItem.groups || []) {\n          walkPages(group.pages || [], tabName, [itemName, group.group], allPages);\n        }\n        if (menuItem.pages) {\n          walkPages(menuItem.pages, tabName, [itemName], allPages);\n        }\n      }\n    } else if (tab.groups) {\n      for (const group of tab.groups) {\n        const groupName = group.group;\n        const isFundamentalsAiAgentsGroup =\n          (tabName === \"Fundamentals\" || tabName === \"Development\") \u0026\u0026\n          (groupName === \"🤖 For AI Agents\" || groupName === \"For AI Agents\");\n        if (SKIP_GROUPS.has(groupName) \u0026\u0026 !isFundamentalsAiAgentsGroup) continue;\n        if (groupName.trim() === \"\") continue;\n\n        walkPages(group.pages || [], tabName, [groupName], allPages);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return allPages;\n}\n\nfunction walkPages(entries, tabName, groupPath, pages) {\n  for (const entry of entries) {\n    if (typeof entry === \"string\") {\n      if (shouldSkip(entry)) continue;\n      if (LLMS_INDEX_SKIP_REFS.has(entry)) continue;\n      // Skip llms-txt/llms-txt-full pages\n      if (entry.includes(\"llms-txt\") || entry.includes(\"llms-full-txt\") || entry.includes(\"llms/\")) continue;\n      // Skip agent-llms pages\n      if (entry.includes(\"agent-llms\")) continue;\n      const mdxPath = resolveMdxPath(entry);\n      if (!mdxPath) continue;\n      const url = pageRefToUrl(entry);\n      const rawContent = fs.readFileSync(mdxPath, \"utf8\");\n      const fm = parseFrontmatter(rawContent);\n      const title =\n        fm?.sidebarTitle || fm?.title || path.basename(entry, \".mdx\");\n      const description = fm?.description || \"\";\n      const content = stripMdxComponents(stripFrontmatter(rawContent));\n\n      pages.push({\n        tab: tabName,\n        groupPath,\n        pageRef: entry,\n        mdxPath,\n        url,\n        title,\n        description,\n        content,\n      });\n    } else if (entry.group \u0026\u0026 entry.pages) {\n      walkPages(entry.pages, tabName, [...groupPath, entry.group], pages);\n    } else if (entry.groups) {\n      for (const g of entry.groups) {\n        walkPages(g.pages || [], tabName, groupPath, pages);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// Organize walked pages by tab → first-level group, preserving nav order.\nfunction groupPagesByTab(pages) {\n  const tabGroups = {};\n  for (const page of pages) {\n    const tab = page.tab;\n    const group = page.groupPath[0] || \"(ungrouped)\";\n    if (!tabGroups[tab]) tabGroups[tab] = {};\n    if (!tabGroups[tab][group]) tabGroups[tab][group] = [];\n    tabGroups[tab][group].push(page);\n  }\n  return tabGroups;\n}\n\n// Organize pages by tab+subsection → inner group, preserving nav order.\n// groupPath[0] is always the subsection (menu item or top-level group);\n// groupPath[1], when present, is the heading group inside it.\nfunction groupPagesBySubsection(pages) {\n  const map = new Map();\n  for (const page of pages) {\n    const sub = page.groupPath[0] || \"(ungrouped)\";\n    const inner = page.groupPath[1] || \"\";\n    const key = subKey(page.tab, sub);\n    if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, new Map());\n    const innerMap = map.get(key);\n    if (!innerMap.has(inner)) innerMap.set(inner, []);\n    innerMap.get(inner).push(page);\n  }\n  return map;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Collect OpenAPI specs from docs.json\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Turn a spec ref like \"call-control-commands/dial.yml\" or a full S3 URL\n// ending in \"ai/inference.yml\" into a human-friendly label\n// (\"Call Control Commands — Dial\"). Falls back to nav-tree context if the\n// immediate label is just \"API Reference\".\nfunction deriveSpecName(ref, ctx) {\n  if (ctx.group \u0026\u0026 ctx.group !== \"API Reference\") return ctx.group;\n  const lastTwo = ref.replace(/\\.(ya?ml|json)$/i, \"\").split(\"/\").slice(-2);\n  const ACRONYMS = new Set([\"ai\", \"api\", \"sip\", \"stt\", \"tts\", \"sms\", \"mms\", \"rcs\", \"uac\", \"ip\", \"url\", \"id\"]);\n  const toTitle = (s) =\u003e\n    s\n      .replace(/[-_]+/g, \" \")\n      .split(\" \")\n      .filter(Boolean)\n      .map((w) =\u003e (ACRONYMS.has(w) ? w.toUpperCase() : w[0].toUpperCase() + w.slice(1)))\n      .join(\" \");\n  if (lastTwo.length === 2) return `${toTitle(lastTwo[0])} — ${toTitle(lastTwo[1])}`;\n  return toTitle(lastTwo[0] || \"API\");\n}\n\n// Walk the navigation tree and collect both legacy `openapi:` objects and\n// Mintlify file-qualified selectors. Each tab is visited one subsection at a\n// time so every spec remains attributed to the navigation context that owns it.\nfunction collectOpenApiSpecs(docsJson) {\n  const specs = [];\n  const specsByContext = new Map();\n\n  const collectOperationRefs = (pages, refs = []) =\u003e {\n    if (!pages) return refs;\n    if (Array.isArray(pages)) {\n      for (const page of pages) collectOperationRefs(page, refs);\n    } else if (\n      typeof pages === \"string\" \u0026\u0026\n      /^(GET|POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE|HEAD|OPTIONS|TRACE)\\s+\\//.test(pages)\n    ) {\n      if (!refs.includes(pages)) refs.push(pages);\n    } else if (typeof pages === \"object\") {\n      collectOperationRefs(pages.pages, refs);\n      collectOperationRefs(pages.groups, refs);\n    }\n    return refs;\n  };\n\n  const addSpec = (specUrl, operationRefs, ctx) =\u003e {\n    const tab = ctx.tab || \"API\";\n    const subsection = ctx.subsection || null;\n    const contextKey = JSON.stringify([specUrl, tab, subsection]);\n    const existing = specsByContext.get(contextKey);\n    if (existing) {\n      if (existing.allOperations || operationRefs.length === 0) {\n        existing.allOperations = true;\n        existing.spec.operationRefs = [];\n      } else {\n        for (const ref of operationRefs) {\n          if (!existing.spec.operationRefs.includes(ref)) existing.spec.operationRefs.push(ref);\n        }\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const spec = {\n      name: deriveSpecName(specUrl, ctx),\n      url: specUrl,\n      tab,\n      subsection,\n      operationRefs: [...operationRefs],\n    };\n    specs.push(spec);\n    specsByContext.set(contextKey, {\n      spec,\n      allOperations: operationRefs.length === 0,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const parseQualifiedSelector = (value) =\u003e {\n    const match = value.match(\n      /^(.+\\.(?:ya?ml|json))\\s+((?:(?:GET|POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE|HEAD|OPTIONS|TRACE)\\s+\\/.+)|(?:webhook\\s+.+))$/i\n    );\n    if (!match) return null;\n    let selector = match[2].trim();\n    const rest = selector.match(/^(GET|POST|PUT|PATCH|DELETE|HEAD|OPTIONS|TRACE)\\s+(\\/.+)$/i);\n    selector = rest\n      ? `${rest[1].toUpperCase()} ${rest[2]}`\n      : `webhook ${selector.replace(/^webhook\\s+/i, \"\")}`;\n    return { specUrl: match[1], selector };\n  };\n\n  const visit = (node, ctx) =\u003e {\n    if (!node) return;\n    if (typeof node === \"string\") {\n      const qualified = parseQualifiedSelector(node);\n      if (qualified) addSpec(qualified.specUrl, [qualified.selector], ctx);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (typeof node !== \"object\") return;\n\n    if (Array.isArray(node)) {\n      for (const child of node) visit(child, ctx);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Update context as we descend (subsection is fixed at the seed level)\n    const nextCtx = { ...ctx };\n    if (node.tab) nextCtx.tab = node.tab;\n    if (node.group \u0026\u0026 !SKIP_GROUPS.has(node.group)) nextCtx.group = node.group;\n    if (node.item \u0026\u0026 !SKIP_GROUPS.has(node.item)) nextCtx.group = node.item;\n\n    // Bail out of skipped subtrees entirely\n    if ((node.group \u0026\u0026 SKIP_GROUPS.has(node.group)) || (node.item \u0026\u0026 SKIP_GROUPS.has(node.item))) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Preserve the legacy object form during direct-source migration.\n    if (typeof node.openapi === \"string\" \u0026\u0026 /\\.(ya?ml|json)$/i.test(node.openapi)) {\n      addSpec(node.openapi, collectOperationRefs(node.pages), nextCtx);\n    }\n\n    for (const value of Object.values(node)) visit(value, nextCtx);\n  };\n\n  for (const tab of navigationTabs(docsJson)) {\n    const tabName = tab.tab;\n    if (tab.menu) {\n      for (const m of tab.menu) {\n        if (m.item \u0026\u0026 SKIP_GROUPS.has(m.item)) continue;\n        visit(m, { tab: tabName, subsection: m.item || null });\n      }\n    } else if (tab.groups) {\n      for (const g of tab.groups) {\n        if (g.group \u0026\u0026 SKIP_GROUPS.has(g.group)) continue;\n        visit(g, { tab: tabName, subsection: g.group || null });\n      }\n    } else {\n      visit(tab, { tab: tabName, subsection: null });\n    }\n  }\n  return specs;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Expand OpenAPI specs into per-operation entries\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst HTTP_METHODS = [\"get\", \"post\", \"put\", \"patch\", \"delete\", \"head\", \"options\", \"trace\"];\n\nasync function loadSpec(ref) {\n  // Local repo paths (e.g. \"openapi/voice-clones.yml\") vs remote URLs.\n  if (ref.startsWith(\"https://\") || ref.startsWith(\"http://\")) {\n    const res = await fetch(ref);\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status} for ${ref}`);\n    return yaml.load(await res.text());\n  }\n  const localPath = path.isAbsolute(ref) ? ref : path.join(REPO_ROOT, ref);\n  return yaml.load(fs.readFileSync(localPath, \"utf8\"));\n}\n\nfunction firstLine(s) {\n  if (!s) return \"\";\n  return s.split(\"\\n\")[0].trim();\n}\n\nfunction collectOperationsFromSpec(doc, sourceUrl, fallbackTag = null) {\n  const operations = [];\n  const pushOp = (op, method, operationPath, selectorRef = null) =\u003e {\n    if (!op || !op.summary) return;\n    // Mintlify derives the published URL from the operation's own tags:\n    // tagged operations live at /api-reference/\u003ctag\u003e/\u003cslug\u003e, untagged ones\n    // at /api-reference/\u003cslug\u003e with no tag segment. The nav-group fallback\n    // tag is only for grouping within our generated files — putting it in\n    // the URL links pages that were never published (the untagged invoice\n    // operations 404ed at /api-reference/invoices/... this way).\n    const ownTag = op.tags?.[0] || null;\n    const rawTag = ownTag || fallbackTag;\n    if (!rawTag) return;\n    const tagSlug = slugifyOpenApi(rawTag);\n    const opSlug = slugifyOpenApi(op.summary);\n    if (!tagSlug || !opSlug) return;\n    operations.push({\n      tag: rawTag,\n      tagSlug,\n      operationId: op.operationId || \"\",\n      title: op.summary,\n      description: firstLine(op.description),\n      url: ownTag ? `/api-reference/${tagSlug}/${opSlug}` : `/api-reference/${opSlug}`,\n      sourceSpec: sourceUrl,\n      operationRef: `${method.toUpperCase()} ${operationPath}`,\n      selectorRef: selectorRef || `${method.toUpperCase()} ${operationPath}`,\n    });\n  };\n\n  for (const [operationPath, methods] of Object.entries(doc.paths || {})) {\n    for (const m of HTTP_METHODS) pushOp(methods?.[m], m, operationPath);\n  }\n  // Mintlify selects OpenAPI 3.1 webhooks by the webhook map key.\n  for (const [operationPath, methods] of Object.entries(doc.webhooks || {})) {\n    for (const m of HTTP_METHODS) {\n      pushOp(methods?.[m], m, operationPath, `webhook ${operationPath}`);\n    }\n  }\n  return operations;\n}\n\nfunction operationsForSpec(doc, spec) {\n  const operations = collectOperationsFromSpec(doc, spec.url, spec.name);\n  const selectors = spec.operationRefs || [];\n  if (selectors.length === 0) return operations;\n\n  return selectors.map((selector) =\u003e {\n    const matches = operations.filter((op) =\u003e op.selectorRef === selector);\n    if (matches.length !== 1) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `Selector ${selector} resolved to ${matches.length} operations in ${spec.url}`\n      );\n    }\n    return matches[0];\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Load every spec once and return:\n *   allOps   – globally deduped operations (for the API Reference shards)\n *   opsByTab – tab name → operations from that tab's specs, deduped within\n *              the tab (a shared spec like Callbacks may appear under\n *              several tabs; each per-section file stays self-contained)\n *   opsBySub – subKey(tab, subsection) → operations from that subsection's\n *              specs, deduped within the subsection\n */\nasync function expandOpenApiOperations(openApiSpecs) {\n  const allOps = [];\n  const seenGlobal = new Set();\n  const opsByTab = new Map();\n  const seenByTab = new Map();\n  const opsBySub = new Map();\n  const seenBySub = new Map();\n  const loadedSpecs = new Map();\n\n  for (const spec of openApiSpecs) {\n    let doc;\n    try {\n      if (!loadedSpecs.has(spec.url)) loadedSpecs.set(spec.url, await loadSpec(spec.url));\n      doc = loadedSpecs.get(spec.url);\n    } catch (e) {\n      throw new Error(`Failed to load ${spec.url}: ${e.message}`, { cause: e });\n    }\n    for (const op of operationsForSpec(doc, spec)) {\n      if (!seenGlobal.has(op.url)) {\n        seenGlobal.add(op.url);\n        allOps.push(op);\n      }\n      const tab = spec.tab;\n      if (!opsByTab.has(tab)) {\n        opsByTab.set(tab, []);\n        seenByTab.set(tab, new Set());\n      }\n      if (!seenByTab.get(tab).has(op.url)) {\n        seenByTab.get(tab).add(op.url);\n        opsByTab.get(tab).push(op);\n      }\n      if (spec.subsection) {\n        const key = subKey(spec.tab, spec.subsection);\n        if (!opsBySub.has(key)) {\n          opsBySub.set(key, []);\n          seenBySub.set(key, new Set());\n        }\n        if (!seenBySub.get(key).has(op.url)) {\n          seenBySub.get(key).add(op.url);\n          opsBySub.get(key).push(op);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return { allOps, opsByTab, opsBySub };\n}\n\n// Group a list of operations by their first tag, preserving order.\nfunction groupOpsByTag(ops) {\n  const byTag = new Map();\n  for (const op of ops) {\n    if (!byTag.has(op.tag)) byTag.set(op.tag, []);\n    byTag.get(op.tag).push(op);\n  }\n  return byTag;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Output generation — root llms.txt (nested index, committed to repo)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction generateRootLlmsIndex(docsJson, includedSubs) {\n  const siteName = docsJson.name || \"Telnyx\";\n  const siteDescription = docsJson.description || \"\";\n  const tabOrder = deriveTabOrder(docsJson);\n\n  let out = \"\";\n  out += `# ${siteName}\\n`;\n  out += `\u003e ${siteDescription}\\n\\n`;\n  out += `Telnyx developer documentation (${BASE_URL}), split into per-section and per-subsection files so agents can load only the relevant product area. Each section links a concise index (page links and descriptions, plus that section's API endpoints) and a \\`-full.txt\\` file carrying complete page content; the nested bullets are focused subsection files.\\n\\n`;\n  out += `## Telnyx developer resources\\n\\n`;\n  out += `- [Telnyx API Documentation](${BASE_URL}/internal/pages/api-reference-overview): REST API reference, endpoints, schemas, and examples by product.\\n`;\n  out += `- [Telnyx API Authentication](${BASE_URL}/docs/development/api-fundamentals/authentication): Authenticate API requests with a Bearer API key.\\n`;\n  out += `- [Telnyx Developer Portal](https://portal.telnyx.com): Manage your account and API keys in Mission Control.\\n`;\n  out += `- [Telnyx MCP Server](${BASE_URL}/docs/development/mcp/remote-mcp): Connect AI agents to the remote Model Context Protocol server.\\n`;\n  out += `- [Telnyx Agent Skills](https://www.skills.sh/team-telnyx/ai): Official Telnyx skills directory with per-skill setup and installation instructions.\\n`;\n  out += `- [Telnyx OpenAPI Specification](${CONSOLIDATED_OPENAPI_SPEC_URL}): Machine-readable REST API contract.\\n\\n`;\n  out += USE_CASES_MD + WHY_TELNYX_MD + CONSTRAINTS_MD + FOR_AGENTS_MD;\n\n  out += `## Documentation sections\\n\\n`;\n  for (const tabName of tabOrder) {\n    const desc = TAB_DESCRIPTIONS[tabName] || \"\";\n    out += `- [${tabName}](${tabIndexUrl(tabName)}): ${desc} ([raw index](${BASE_URL}${rawAssetRoute(tabName)}), [full content](${tabFullUrl(tabName)}), [raw full content](${BASE_URL}${rawAssetRoute(tabName, undefined, true)}))\\n`;\n    for (const subName of includedSubs.get(tabName) || []) {\n      out += `  - [${subName}](${subIndexUrl(tabName, subName)}) ([raw index](${BASE_URL}${rawAssetRoute(tabName, subName)}), [full](${subFullUrl(tabName, subName)}), [raw full](${BASE_URL}${rawAssetRoute(tabName, subName, true)}))\\n`;\n    }\n  }\n  out += \"\\n\";\n\n  // Pricing — agents need discoverable pricing to relay recommendations.\n  out += `## Pricing\\n\\n`;\n  out += `- [Pricing overview](${BASE_URL}/pricing.md): Pay-as-you-go rates for voice, messaging, phone numbers, wireless, AI inference, networking, and storage — full rate tables at https://telnyx.com/pricing and machine-readable pricing at https://telnyx.com/pricing.md.\\n\\n`;\n\n  // Reliability \u0026 Status — status page, machine-readable incident data, and\n  // SLA so agents source uptime/incident info from Telnyx, not aggregators.\n  out += RELIABILITY_MD;\n\n  // OpenAPI Specification — single link to the consolidated spec.\n  out += `## OpenAPI Specification\\n\\n`;\n  out += `- [Telnyx OpenAPI Specification (consolidated)](${CONSOLIDATED_OPENAPI_SPEC_URL}): Single OpenAPI 3.1 JSON covering every Telnyx REST API surface — paths, schemas, and webhooks.\\n\\n`;\n\n  return out;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Output generation — per-section and per-subsection intermediate files\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction appendApiReference(out, label, ops) {\n  if (!ops || ops.length === 0) return out;\n  const byTag = groupOpsByTag(ops);\n  out += `## API Reference (${label})\\n\\n`;\n  for (const [tag, tagOps] of byTag) {\n    out += `### ${tag}\\n\\n`;\n    for (const op of tagOps) {\n      // First line of the operation's spec description — gives agents a\n      // quotable summary per endpoint. Markdown links are flattened to\n      // their text so truncation can't leave broken syntax. Shards skip\n      // these to stay \u003c50K.\n      const d = shortDescription(\n        (op.description || \"\").replace(/\\[([^\\]]*)\\]\\([^)]*\\)/g, \"$1\"),\n        160\n      );\n      out += d\n        ? `- [${op.title}](${BASE_URL}${op.url}.md): ${d}\\n`\n        : `- [${op.title}](${BASE_URL}${op.url}.md)\\n`;\n    }\n    out += \"\\n\";\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nfunction generateTabLlmsTxt(tabName, groups, tabOps, tabSubs) {\n  const desc = TAB_DESCRIPTIONS[tabName] || \"\";\n  let out = \"\";\n  out += `# Telnyx ${tabName} — Documentation Index\\n`;\n  out += `\u003e ${desc}. One section of the Telnyx developer docs (${BASE_URL}).\\n`;\n  out += `\u003e This file: ${tabIndexUrl(tabName)} · Root index: ${BASE_URL}/llms.txt\\n\\n`;\n\n  if (tabSubs \u0026\u0026 tabSubs.length \u003e 0) {\n    out += `## Subsections\\n\\n`;\n    out += `Focused per-subsection index files:\\n\\n`;\n    for (const subName of tabSubs) {\n      out += `- [${subName}](${subIndexUrl(tabName, subName)})\\n`;\n    }\n    out += \"\\n\";\n  }\n\n  for (const [groupName, groupPages] of Object.entries(groups)) {\n    if (Object.keys(groups).length \u003e 1) {\n      out += `## ${groupName}\\n\\n`;\n    }\n    for (const page of groupPages) {\n      const d = shortDescription(page.description);\n      out += d\n        ? `- [${page.title}](${toMdUrl(page.url)}): ${d}\\n`\n        : `- [${page.title}](${toMdUrl(page.url)})\\n`;\n    }\n    out += \"\\n\";\n  }\n\n  return appendApiReference(out, tabName, tabOps);\n}\n\nfunction generateTabLlmsFullTxt(tabName, groups, tabOps, tabSubs) {\n  const desc = TAB_DESCRIPTIONS[tabName] || \"\";\n  let out = \"\";\n  out += `# Telnyx ${tabName} — Full Documentation\\n`;\n  out += `\u003e ${desc}. Complete page content for the ${tabName} section of the Telnyx developer docs (${BASE_URL}).\\n`;\n  out += `\u003e This file: ${tabFullUrl(tabName)} · Root index: ${BASE_URL}/llms.txt\\n\\n`;\n\n  if (tabSubs \u0026\u0026 tabSubs.length \u003e 0) {\n    out += `## Subsections\\n\\n`;\n    out += `Focused per-subsection full-content files:\\n\\n`;\n    for (const subName of tabSubs) {\n      out += `- [${subName}](${subFullUrl(tabName, subName)})\\n`;\n    }\n    out += \"\\n\";\n  }\n\n  for (const [groupName, groupPages] of Object.entries(groups)) {\n    out += `## ${groupName}\\n\\n`;\n    for (const page of groupPages) {\n      out += `### ${page.title}\\n\\n`;\n      if (page.url) {\n        out += `\u003e Source: ${toMdUrl(page.url)}\\n\\n`;\n      }\n      out += page.content;\n      out += \"\\n\\n---\\n\\n\";\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Endpoint pages are generated from OpenAPI specs (no MDX source), so the\n  // full file links them rather than inlining.\n  return appendApiReference(out, tabName, tabOps);\n}\n\nfunction generateSubLlmsTxt(tabName, subName, innerGroups, subOps) {\n  let out = \"\";\n  out += `# Telnyx ${tabName}: ${subName} — Documentation Index\\n`;\n  out += `\u003e ${subName} documentation within the ${tabName} section of the Telnyx developer docs (${BASE_URL}).\\n`;\n  out += `\u003e This file: ${subIndexUrl(tabName, subName)} · Root index: ${BASE_URL}/llms.txt\\n\\n`;\n\n  const namedInner = [...innerGroups.keys()].filter((n) =\u003e n);\n  const useHeadings = namedInner.length \u003e 1 || innerGroups.size \u003e 1;\n  for (const [innerName, innerPages] of innerGroups) {\n    if (useHeadings \u0026\u0026 innerName) {\n      out += `## ${innerName}\\n\\n`;\n    }\n    for (const page of innerPages) {\n      const d = shortDescription(page.description);\n      out += d\n        ? `- [${page.title}](${toMdUrl(page.url)}): ${d}\\n`\n        : `- [${page.title}](${toMdUrl(page.url)})\\n`;\n    }\n    out += \"\\n\";\n  }\n\n  return appendApiReference(out, subName, subOps);\n}\n\nfunction generateSubLlmsFullTxt(tabName, subName, innerGroups, subOps) {\n  let out = \"\";\n  out += `# Telnyx ${tabName}: ${subName} — Full Documentation\\n`;\n  out += `\u003e Complete page content for ${subName} (${tabName} section) of the Telnyx developer docs (${BASE_URL}).\\n`;\n  out += `\u003e This file: ${subFullUrl(tabName, subName)} · Root index: ${BASE_URL}/llms.txt\\n\\n`;\n\n  for (const [innerName, innerPages] of innerGroups) {\n    if (innerName) {\n      out += `## ${innerName}\\n\\n`;\n    }\n    for (const page of innerPages) {\n      out += `### ${page.title}\\n\\n`;\n      if (page.url) {\n        out += `\u003e Source: ${toMdUrl(page.url)}\\n\\n`;\n      }\n      out += page.content;\n      out += \"\\n\\n---\\n\\n\";\n    }\n  }\n\n  return appendApiReference(out, subName, subOps);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Output generation — site-wide llms-full.txt (committed to repo)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction generateLlmsFullTxt(docsJson, includedSubs) {\n  const siteName = docsJson.name || \"Telnyx\";\n  const siteDescription = docsJson.description || \"\";\n  const tabOrder = deriveTabOrder(docsJson);\n\n  let out = \"\";\n  out += `# ${siteName} — Full Documentation\\n`;\n  out += `\u003e ${siteDescription}\\n\\n`;\n  out += `Complete Telnyx developer documentation, split into per-area full-content files (the combined corpus is ~3.4M characters — far past single-request agent limits). Each link below carries the complete page content for its area; this file holds authentication, quickstart examples, and the map. Lightweight per-area indexes are listed in [llms.txt](${BASE_URL}/llms.txt).\\n\\n`;\n  out += USE_CASES_MD + CONSTRAINTS_MD + FOR_AGENTS_MD + AUTH_QUICKSTART_MD;\n\n  out += `## Per-area full documentation\\n\\n`;\n  for (const tabName of tabOrder) {\n    out += `- [${tabName} — full content](${tabFullUrl(tabName)}): ${TAB_DESCRIPTIONS[tabName] || \"\"}\\n`;\n    for (const subName of includedSubs.get(tabName) || []) {\n      out += `  - [${subName} — full content](${subFullUrl(tabName, subName)})\\n`;\n    }\n  }\n  out += \"\\n\" + RELIABILITY_MD;\n  out += `## OpenAPI Specification\\n\\n`;\n  out += `- [Telnyx OpenAPI Specification (consolidated)](${CONSOLIDATED_OPENAPI_SPEC_URL}): Single OpenAPI 3.1 JSON covering every Telnyx REST API surface.\\n\\n`;\n  return out;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Mintlify-native llms pages (committed) — one pair per product/subsection\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction landingBase(tabName, subName) {\n  return `${TAB_SLUGS[tabName]}-${subsectionSlug(subName)}`;\n}\n\nfunction landingPageRefs(tabName, subName) {\n  // Preserve the public machine-readable routes from the former standalone\n  // Development and Other APIs tabs after they move under Fundamentals.\n  if (tabName === \"Fundamentals\" \u0026\u0026 subName === \"Development\") {\n    return [\"docs/development/llms-txt/index\", \"docs/development/llms-txt-full/index\"];\n  }\n  if (tabName === \"Fundamentals\" \u0026\u0026 subName === \"Other APIs\") {\n    return [\n      \"docs/development/llms/other-apis-llms-txt/index\",\n      \"docs/development/llms/other-apis-llms-full-txt/index\",\n    ];\n  }\n  const base = landingBase(tabName, subName);\n  // Refs MUST end in /index. Mintlify's nav indexer only reads a page's\n  // frontmatter (title/sidebarTitle) when the docs.json ref explicitly\n  // targets the index file; a bare directory ref makes the sidebar fall\n  // back to humanizing the slug (\"Calling voice api llms txt\"). The\n  // known-good entries (docs/development/agent-skills/index, the MCP pages)\n  // all use the explicit /index form. URLs are unchanged — Mintlify\n  // serves \u003cdir\u003e/index at /\u003cdir\u003e.\n  return [\n    `docs/development/llms/${base}-llms-txt/index`,\n    `docs/development/llms/${base}-llms-full-txt/index`,\n  ];\n}\n\n// Generated llms content is valid Markdown but may contain JSX-like tags or\n// bare object examples that MDX would interpret. Preserve Markdown structure\n// while escaping only MDX-significant characters outside fenced code blocks.\nfunction makeGeneratedContentMdxSafe(content) {\n  let fence = null;\n  return content\n    .split(\"\\n\")\n    .map((line) =\u003e {\n      line = line.replace(/\\t/g, \"  \").replace(/[ \\t]+$/, \"\");\n      const marker = line.match(/^\\s*(`{3,}|~{3,})/);\n      if (marker) {\n        if (!fence) fence = marker[1][0];\n        else if (fence === marker[1][0]) fence = null;\n        return line;\n      }\n      if (fence) return line;\n      return line\n        .replace(/\u003c/g, \"\u0026lt;\")\n        .replace(/{/g, \"\u0026#123;\")\n        .replace(/}/g, \"\u0026#125;\");\n    })\n    .join(\"\\n\");\n}\n\nfunction generatedMdxPage(title, sidebarTitle, description, content) {\n  return `---\ntitle: ${JSON.stringify(title)}\nsidebarTitle: ${JSON.stringify(sidebarTitle)}\ndescription: ${JSON.stringify(description)}\n---\n\n${makeGeneratedContentMdxSafe(content).trimEnd()}\n`;\n}\n\nfunction generatedRedirectMdxPage(title, sidebarTitle, description) {\n  return `---\ntitle: ${JSON.stringify(title)}\nsidebarTitle: ${JSON.stringify(sidebarTitle)}\ndescription: ${JSON.stringify(description)}\n---\n`;\n}\n\n\nfunction generateLandingTxtMdx(tabName, subName) {\n  return generatedRedirectMdxPage(\n    `${subName} llms.txt`,\n    `${subName} llms txt`,\n    `Machine-readable documentation index for ${subName} (${tabName}) — guide pages and API endpoints for AI agents and LLMs`\n  );\n}\n\nfunction generateLandingFullMdx(tabName, subName) {\n  return generatedRedirectMdxPage(\n    `${subName} llms-full.txt`,\n    `${subName} llms full txt`,\n    `Complete machine-readable documentation content for ${subName} (${tabName}) for AI agents and LLMs`\n  );\n}\n\nfunction tabLandingRefs(tabName) {\n  const slug = TAB_SLUGS[tabName];\n  // /index suffix required — see landingPageRefs().\n  return [\n    `docs/development/llms/${slug}-llms-txt/index`,\n    `docs/development/llms/${slug}-llms-full-txt/index`,\n  ];\n}\n\nfunction generateTabLandingTxtMdx(tabName) {\n  return generatedRedirectMdxPage(\n    `${tabName} llms.txt`,\n    `${tabName} llms txt`,\n    `Machine-readable documentation index for the ${tabName} section — guide pages and API endpoints for AI agents and LLMs`\n  );\n}\n\nfunction generateTabLandingFullMdx(tabName) {\n  return generatedRedirectMdxPage(\n    `${tabName} llms-full.txt`,\n    `${tabName} llms full txt`,\n    `Complete machine-readable documentation content for the ${tabName} section for AI agents and LLMs`\n  );\n}\n\n// Code examples — the full snippet (33+ KB) no longer fits inside the 50K\n// root-index budget, so it serves as its own hidden Mintlify-native page and\n// the root index carries a one-entry section pointing at it. llms-full.txt\n// still inlines the complete snippet.\nconst CODE_EXAMPLES_PAGE_REF = \"docs/development/llms/code-examples/index\";\nconst CODE_EXAMPLES_PAGE_URL = `${BASE_URL}/docs/development/llms/code-examples.md`;\nconst CODE_EXAMPLES_REPO_URL =\n  \"https://github.com/team-telnyx/telnyx-code-examples\";\n\nfunction codeExamplesRootSection(snippet) {\n  const exampleCount = (snippet.match(/^- \\[/gm) || []).length;\n  return (\n    `## Code Examples\\n\\n` +\n    `- [Code examples index](${CODE_EXAMPLES_PAGE_URL}): ${exampleCount} production-ready Python examples covering the full Telnyx platform — each ships app.py, README, tutorial guide, and endpoint reference. Source repository: ${CODE_EXAMPLES_REPO_URL}\\n`\n  );\n}\n\nfunction writeCodeExamplesPage(snippet) {\n  const dir = path.join(LANDING_DIR, \"code-examples\");\n  fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });\n  fs.writeFileSync(\n    path.join(dir, \"index.mdx\"),\n    generatedMdxPage(\n      \"Code Examples llms.txt\",\n      \"Code examples llms txt\",\n      \"Machine-readable index of production-ready Telnyx code examples for AI agents and LLMs\",\n      snippet\n    ),\n    \"utf8\"\n  );\n}\n\nfunction tabLandingDirs(tabName) {\n  const slug = TAB_SLUGS[tabName];\n  return [\n    path.join(LANDING_DIR, `${slug}-llms-txt`),\n    path.join(LANDING_DIR, `${slug}-llms-full-txt`),\n  ];\n}\n\nfunction landingPageDirs(tabName, subName) {\n  return landingPageRefs(tabName, subName).map((ref) =\u003e\n    path.join(REPO_ROOT, ref.replace(/\\/index$/, \"\"))\n  );\n}\n\nfunction writeLandingPages(includedSubs) {\n  let count = 0;\n  for (const [tabName, subs] of includedSubs) {\n    if (subs.length === 0) continue;\n\n    // Every top-level product gets a Mintlify-native index and full-content\n    // page. These replace the bucket-hosted /llms/\u003cproduct\u003e.txt artifacts.\n    const slug = TAB_SLUGS[tabName];\n    const [tabTxtDir, tabFullDir] = tabLandingDirs(tabName);\n    fs.mkdirSync(tabTxtDir, { recursive: true });\n    fs.mkdirSync(tabFullDir, { recursive: true });\n    fs.writeFileSync(\n      path.join(tabTxtDir, \"index.mdx\"),\n      generateTabLandingTxtMdx(tabName),\n      \"utf8\"\n    );\n    fs.writeFileSync(\n      path.join(tabFullDir, \"index.mdx\"),\n      generateTabLandingFullMdx(tabName),\n      \"utf8\"\n    );\n    count += 2;\n\n    for (const subName of subs) {\n      const [txtDir, fullDir] = landingPageDirs(tabName, subName);\n      fs.mkdirSync(txtDir, { recursive: true });\n      fs.mkdirSync(fullDir, { recursive: true });\n      fs.writeFileSync(\n        path.join(txtDir, \"index.mdx\"),\n        generateLandingTxtMdx(tabName, subName),\n        \"utf8\"\n      );\n      fs.writeFileSync(\n        path.join(fullDir, \"index.mdx\"),\n        generateLandingFullMdx(tabName, subName),\n        \"utf8\"\n      );\n      count += 2;\n    }\n  }\n  return count;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// docs.json sidebar updater — \"For AI Agents\" group in every subsection\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst AI_AGENTS_GROUP = \"For AI Agents\";\n// Names to recognize when removing previously generated groups — includes the\n// legacy emoji-prefixed form so a rename never leaves duplicates behind.\nconst AI_AGENTS_GROUP_NAMES = new Set([AI_AGENTS_GROUP, \"🤖 For AI Agents\"]);\n\n// Tabs whose llms sidebar is consolidated into their single top-level\n// \"For AI Agents\" group: only the tab-level landing pair is listed in the\n// navigation. Per-subsection pages are still written and served — Mintlify\n// renders pages absent from docs.json as hidden pages (reachable by URL and\n// their .md variants, verified live with /pricing and /compare); they are\n// only left out of the sidebar, search, and sitemap. The tab-level pair\n// already bundles every subsection's pages and endpoints inline.\nconst SIDEBAR_CONSOLIDATED_TABS = new Set([\"Agent SDK\"]);\n\nfunction consolidatedTabLandingRefs(tabName) {\n  return tabLandingRefs(tabName);\n}\n\n// Per-subsection pages of consolidated tabs are deliberately hidden: they\n// exist in the repo and serve at their URLs, but must not appear in\n// docs.json navigation. The same applies to the tab-level landing pairs of\n// non-consolidated tabs: each subsection sidebar lists only its own pair,\n// and the tab pair stays reachable through the root llms.txt index.\n// check-llms-native-routes.js enforces both sides.\nfunction isHiddenLlmsPageRef(ref) {\n  if (ref === CODE_EXAMPLES_PAGE_REF) return true;\n  for (const tabName of SIDEBAR_CONSOLIDATED_TABS) {\n    if (tabLandingRefs(tabName).includes(ref)) continue;\n    if (ref.startsWith(`docs/development/llms/${TAB_SLUGS[tabName]}-`)) return true;\n  }\n  for (const tabName of Object.keys(TAB_SLUGS)) {\n    if (SIDEBAR_CONSOLIDATED_TABS.has(tabName)) continue;\n    if (tabLandingRefs(tabName).includes(ref)) return true;\n  }\n  return false;\n}\n\nfunction isGeneratedLlmsPageRef(page) {\n  return (\n    typeof page === \"string\" \u0026\u0026\n    (page.startsWith(\"docs/development/llms/\") ||\n      page === \"docs/development/llms-txt/index\" ||\n      page === \"docs/development/llms-txt-full/index\")\n  );\n}\n\nfunction routeForLlmsPageRef(ref) {\n  return `/${ref.replace(/\\/index$/, \"\")}`;\n}\n\nfunction rawAssetRoute(tabName, subName, full = false) {\n  const tabSlug = TAB_SLUGS[tabName];\n  const suffix = full ? \"-full.txt\" : \".txt\";\n  if (tabName === \"Fundamentals\" \u0026\u0026 subName === \"Development\") {\n    return `/public/llms/development${suffix}`;\n  }\n  if (tabName === \"Fundamentals\" \u0026\u0026 subName === \"Other APIs\") {\n    return `/public/llms/other-apis${suffix}`;\n  }\n  return subName\n    ? `/public/llms/${tabSlug}/${subsectionSlug(subName)}${suffix}`\n    : `/public/llms/${tabSlug}${suffix}`;\n}\n\n// Mintlify sidebar groups accept page references, not arbitrary href objects.\n// Keep each generated page registered for publication, then redirect its HTML\n// route to a distinct static text asset. Redirecting to its `.md` export is\n// unsafe because Mintlify reapplies that rule to `.md` and creates `.md.md`.\nfunction rawLlmsRedirects(includedSubs) {\n  const redirects = [];\n  for (const [tabName, subs] of includedSubs) {\n    if (subs.length === 0) continue;\n    const tabRefs = tabLandingRefs(tabName);\n    redirects.push(\n      { source: routeForLlmsPageRef(tabRefs[0]), destination: rawAssetRoute(tabName) },\n      { source: routeForLlmsPageRef(tabRefs[1]), destination: rawAssetRoute(tabName, null, true) },\n    );\n    for (const subName of subs) {\n      const refs = landingPageRefs(tabName, subName);\n      redirects.push(\n        { source: routeForLlmsPageRef(refs[0]), destination: rawAssetRoute(tabName, subName) },\n        { source: routeForLlmsPageRef(refs[1]), destination: rawAssetRoute(tabName, subName, true) },\n      );\n    }\n  }\n  return redirects;\n}\n\nfunction isGeneratedRawLlmsRedirect({ source, destination }) {\n  return (\n    typeof source === \"string\" \u0026\u0026\n    typeof destination === \"string\" \u0026\u0026\n    (destination === `${source}.md` || /^\\/(?:public\\/)?llms\\/.+\\.txt$/.test(destination)) \u0026\u0026\n    (/^\\/docs\\/development\\/llms\\//.test(source) ||\n      source === \"/docs/development/llms-txt\" ||\n      source === \"/docs/development/llms-txt-full\")\n  );\n}\n\nfunction rawLlmsAssetContent(source) {\n  const normalized = source\n    .split(\"\\n\")\n    .map((line) =\u003e line.replace(/\\t/g, \"  \").trimEnd())\n    .join(\"\\n\")\n    .trimEnd();\n  // Mintlify serves nested static .txt files without a charset parameter.\n  // A UTF-8 BOM preserves Unicode when browsers otherwise assume Windows-1252.\n  return `\\uFEFF${normalized}\\n`;\n}\n\nfunction writeRawLlmsAssets(includedSubs) {\n  fs.rmSync(RAW_LLMS_DIR, { recursive: true, force: true });\n  fs.mkdirSync(RAW_LLMS_DIR, { recursive: true });\n  let count = 0;\n\n  for (const [tabName, subs] of includedSubs) {\n    if (subs.length === 0) continue;\n    const tabSlug = TAB_SLUGS[tabName];\n    for (const fileName of [`${tabSlug}.txt`, `${tabSlug}-full.txt`]) {\n      const source = fs.readFileSync(path.join(LLMS_OUT_DIR, fileName), \"utf8\");\n      fs.writeFileSync(\n        path.join(RAW_LLMS_DIR, fileName),\n        rawLlmsAssetContent(source),\n        \"utf8\",\n      );\n      count++;\n    }\n    for (const subName of subs) {\n      const subSlug = subsectionSlug(subName);\n      for (const full of [false, true]) {\n        const fileName = `${subSlug}${full ? \"-full\" : \"\"}.txt`;\n        const source = fs.readFileSync(path.join(LLMS_OUT_DIR, tabSlug, fileName), \"utf8\");\n        const destination = path.join(REPO_ROOT, rawAssetRoute(tabName, subName, full).slice(1));\n        fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(destination), { recursive: true });\n        fs.writeFileSync(\n          destination,\n          rawLlmsAssetContent(source),\n          \"utf8\",\n        );\n        count++;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return count;\n}\n\n// Idempotent: generated per-subsection AI-agents entries are rebuilt on every\n// run, while authored entries such as MCP and Agent Skills are preserved.\nfunction updateDocsJsonSidebar(includedSubs, docsJsonPath = DOCS_JSON_PATH) {\n  const raw = fs.readFileSync(docsJsonPath, \"utf8\");\n  const docsJson = JSON.parse(raw);\n  let updated = 0;\n\n  // Consolidated tabs list only the tab-level landing pair; per-subsection\n  // pages stay published as hidden pages, out of the nav. Preserve\n  // hand-managed entries such as the Development MCP and Agent Skills pages.\n  // `entries` holds group objects (tab.groups, Home's groups) or the mixed\n  // page list of the Home \"Other APIs\" group.\n  const rebuildConsolidatedGroup = (entries, tabName, additionalAuthoredPages = []) =\u003e {\n    const generatedPages = consolidatedTabLandingRefs(tabName);\n    const existing = entries.find(\n      (entry) =\u003e entry \u0026\u0026 typeof entry === \"object\" \u0026\u0026 AI_AGENTS_GROUP_NAMES.has(entry.group)\n    );\n    const authoredPages = [\n      ...(existing?.pages || []).filter((page) =\u003e !isGeneratedLlmsPageRef(page)),\n      ...additionalAuthoredPages,\n    ];\n    if (existing) {\n      existing.group = AI_AGENTS_GROUP;\n      existing.pages = [...authoredPages, ...generatedPages];\n    } else {\n      entries.push({ group: AI_AGENTS_GROUP, pages: [...authoredPages, ...generatedPages] });\n    }\n    updated++;\n  };\n\n  for (const tab of docsJson.navigation?.tabs || []) {\n    const tabName = tab.tab;\n    const subs = new Set(includedSubs.get(tabName) || []);\n    const consolidated = SIDEBAR_CONSOLIDATED_TABS.has(tabName);\n\n    if (tab.menu) {\n      for (const m of tab.menu) {\n        const n = m.item;\n        if (!n || !subs.has(n)) continue;\n        const existing = (m.groups || []).find((g) =\u003e AI_AGENTS_GROUP_NAMES.has(g.group));\n        const authoredPages = (existing?.pages || []).filter(\n          (page) =\u003e !isGeneratedLlmsPageRef(page)\n        );\n        m.groups = (m.groups || []).filter((g) =\u003e !AI_AGENTS_GROUP_NAMES.has(g.group));\n        if (consolidated) continue;\n        m.groups.push({\n          group: AI_AGENTS_GROUP,\n          pages: [...authoredPages, ...landingPageRefs(tabName, n)],\n        });\n        updated++;\n      }\n    } else if (tab.groups) {\n      for (const g of tab.groups) {\n        const n = g.group;\n        if (!n || !subs.has(n)) continue;\n        g.pages = (g.pages || []).filter(\n          (p) =\u003e !(p \u0026\u0026 typeof p === \"object\" \u0026\u0026 AI_AGENTS_GROUP_NAMES.has(p.group))\n        );\n        if (consolidated) continue;\n        g.pages.push({ group: AI_AGENTS_GROUP, pages: landingPageRefs(tabName, n) });\n        updated++;\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Consolidated tabs list only the tab-level landing pair; per-subsection\n    // pages stay published as hidden pages, out of the nav.\n    if (consolidated \u0026\u0026 tab.groups \u0026\u0026 subs.size \u003e 0) {\n      rebuildConsolidatedGroup(tab.groups, tabName);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Products division: every nested product tab is a legacy subsection;\n  // Home's groups are the Fundamentals family's subsections. Mutates the\n  // real docs.json nodes, so this walks the products shape directly instead\n  // of the read-only navigationTabs() view.\n  if (!docsJson.navigation?.tabs) {\n    for (const product of docsJson.navigation?.products || []) {\n      if (product.product === HOME_PRODUCT) {\n        const family = PRODUCT_TO_TAB[HOME_PRODUCT];\n        const subs = new Set(includedSubs.get(family) || []);\n        for (const g of product.groups || []) {\n          const n = g.group;\n          if (!n || !subs.has(n)) continue;\n          const existing = (g.pages || []).find(\n            (p) =\u003e p \u0026\u0026 typeof p === \"object\" \u0026\u0026 AI_AGENTS_GROUP_NAMES.has(p.group)\n          );\n          const authoredPages = (existing?.pages || []).filter(\n            (page)
