edit) ---\";\nconst END_MARKER = \"// --- VOICE DECODE END ---\";\n\nconst updateVoiceExplorer = () =\u003e {\n  if (!fs.existsSync(dataPath)) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `Unable to find voices data at ${dataPath}. for (const line of sectionText.split(\"\\n\")) {\n    const h3 = line.match(/^### (.+)$/);\n    if (h3) {\n      subs.set(current, buffer.join(\"\\n\").trim());\n      current = h3[1].trim();\n      buffer = [];\n      continue;\n    }\n    buffer.push(line);\n  }\n  subs.set(current, buffer.join(\"\\n\").trim());\n  return subs;\n}\n\nfunction resolveSpec(lang, cfg, sections, spec) {\n  const rewriteSplitPageLinks(text, lang, cfg) {\n  for (const [source, destination] of Object.entries(cfg.linkRewrites ?? {})) {\n    text = text.replaceAll(`](${source})`, `](${destination})`);\n  }\n\n  let inFence = false;\n  return text\n    .split(\"\\n\")\n    .map((line) =\u003e {\n      if (line.trimStart().startsWith(\"```\")) {\n        inFence = !inFence;\n        return line;\n      }\n const outDir = path.join(SDK_SHARED, cfg.outDir);\n  fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });\n  for (const staleFile of STALE_TOPIC_FILES) {\n    fs.rmSync(path.join(outDir, staleFile), { force: [\"trafficpolicyprofiles\", \"Traffic Policy Profiles\", \"Calling\"],\n  ].map(([alias, name, section]) =\u003e [alias, { name, section }]),\n);\n\nconst GO_RESOURCE_PRESENTATION = new Map(\n  [\n    [\"brand\", \"10DLC — Brands\"],\n    [\"campaign\", \"10DLC — Campaigns\"],\n    [\"campaignbuilder\", \"10DLC — Campaign Builder\"],\n    [\"partnercampaigns\", \"10DLC — Partner Campaigns\"],\n ].map(([alias, name]) =\u003e [alias, { name, section: \"Messaging\" }]),\n);\n\nfunction resourcePresentation(lang, name) {\n  // The generated clients expose some products through several endpoint-\n  // derived properties. Present those properties as one developer concept\n  // while retaining every language's exact call syntax in the method table.\n  const normalized = name.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, \"\").toLowerCase();\n  if (normalized.startsWith(\"globalip\")) {\n    return { name: \"Global IP\", section: \"Compute\" };\n  }\n  if (lang === \"golang\" \u0026\u0026 name === \"(client root)\") {\n    return { name: \"Storage\", section: \"Storage\" };\n  }\n  if (lang === \"golang\" \u0026\u0026 GO_RESOURCE_PRESENTATION.has(normalized)) {\n    return GO_RESOURCE_PRESENTATION.get(normalized);\n  }\n  return RESOURCE_PRESENTATION.get(normalized) ?? { name };\n}\n\nfunction buildResourceMap(lang, cfg, opMaps) {\n  const { byRef: urlByRef, tabByRef, tabOrder } = opMaps;\n  const apiMdPath = path.join(SDK_SHARED, cfg.apiMd);\n  const text = fs.readFileSync(apiMdPath, \"utf8\");\n\n  // api.md method entries look like:\n  //   - \u003ccode title=\"post /calls\"\u003eclient.calls.\u003ca href=\"...\"\u003edial\u003c/a\u003e(...)\u003c/code\u003e\n  const methodRe =\n    /\u003ccode title=\"(\\w+) ([^\"]+)\"\u003e([^\u003c]*?)\u003ca href=\"[^\"]*\"\u003e([\\w\\\\*]+)\u003c\\/a\u003e/g;\n\n  const groups = new Map(); // presented resource -\u003e [{verb, path, call}]\n  const sectionOverrides = new Map(); // presented resource -\u003e curated product tab\n  let match;\n  while ((match = methodRe.exec(text)) !== null) {\n    const [, verb, apiPath, rawPrefix, rawMethod] = match;\n    // api.md markdown-escapes underscores in identifiers; depending on the\n    // escaping pass they surface as `\\_` or a bare `*` — restore both.\n    const unescape = (s) =\u003e s.replace(/\\\\_/g, \"_\").replace(/\\*/g, \"_\");\n    const prefix = unescape(rawPrefix);\n    const method = unescape(rawMethod);\n    const call = `${prefix}${method}()`;\n    const separator = prefix.includes(\"-\u003e\") ? \"-\u003e\" : \".\";\n    const segments = prefix\n      .split(separator)\n      .map((segment) =\u003e segment.replace(/\\(\\)$/g, \"\"))\n      .filter(Boolean);\n    const rawTop = segments.length \u003e 1 ? segments[1] : \"(client root)\";\n    const presentation = resourcePresentation(lang, rawTop);\n    const top = presentation.name;\n    if (presentation.section) sectionOverrides.set(top, presentation.section);\n    if (!groups.has(top)) groups.set(top, []);\n    groups.get(top).push({ verb: verb.toUpperCase(), path: apiPath, call });\n  }\n\n  if (groups.size === 0) {\n    throw new Error(`${lang}: no methods parsed from ${cfg.apiMd}`);\n  }\n\n  // Assign each resource to a product area by majority vote over the\n  // docs.json navigation tab of its methods; resources whose operations are\n  // not published in the API reference fall into \"Legacy APIs\".\n  const OTHER = \"Legacy APIs\";\n  const sectionOf = new Map(); // resource -\u003e product tab\n  for (const [top, methods] of groups) {\n    const votes = new Map();\n    for (const m of methods) {\n      const tab = tabByRef.get(`${m.verb} ${m.path}`);\n      if (tab) votes.set(tab, (votes.get(tab) || 0) + 1);\n    }\n    let best = sectionOverrides.get(top) ?? OTHER;\n    let bestCount = 0;\n    if (!sectionOverrides.has(top)) {\n      for (const [tab, n] of votes) {\n        if (n \u003e bestCount) {\n          best = tab;\n          bestCount = n;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    sectionOf.set(top, best);\n  }\n  const sectionOrder = tabOrder\n    .filter((tab) =\u003e [...sectionOf.values()].includes(tab))\n    .concat([...sectionOf.values()].includes(OTHER) ? [OTHER] : []);\n\n  const parts = [BANNER(cfg.apiMd).trim(), \"\"];\n  const total = [...groups.values()].reduce((n, m) =\u003e n + m.length, 0);\n  parts.push(\n    `This SDK exposes ${total} methods across ${groups.size} resource groups. ` +\n      `Search by resource, SDK method, HTTP verb, or REST endpoint, or narrow ` +\n      `the directory by product area. Linked endpoints open the ` +\n      `[API reference](/api-reference) with parameters, responses, and a ` +\n      `ready-to-run code sample.`,\n    \"\",\n  );\n\n  // Each product area is an H2 section (so it lands in the on-page table of\n  // contents) holding one collapsed accordion per resource. Endpoints link\n  // to their API-reference page when the operation is published there.\n  let unlinked = 0;\n  for (const section of sectionOrder) {\n    const sectionResources = [...groups.keys()]\n      .filter((top) =\u003e sectionOf.get(top) === section)\n      .sort((a, b) =\u003e a.localeCompare(b));\n    parts.push(`## ${section}`, \"\");\n    parts.push(\"\u003cAccordionGroup\u003e\", \"\");\n    for (const top of sectionResources) {\n      const methods = groups.get(top);\n      const label = methods.length === 1 ? \"method\" : \"methods\";\n      parts.push(`\u003cAccordion title=\"${top} — ${methods.length} ${label}\"\u003e`, \"\");\n      parts.push(\"| Method | Endpoint |\", \"| --- | --- |\");\n      for (const m of methods) {\n        const ref = `${m.verb} ${m.path}`;\n        const url = urlByRef.get(ref);\n        if (!url) unlinked += 1;\n        const endpoint = url ? `[\\`${ref}\\`](${url})` : `\\`${ref}\\``;\n        parts.push(`| \\`${m.call}\\` | ${endpoint} |`);\n      }\n      parts.push(\"\", \"\u003c/Accordion\u003e\", \"\");\n    }\n    parts.push(\"\u003c/AccordionGroup\u003e\", \"\");\n  }\n\n  const outDir = path.join(SDK_SHARED, cfg.outDir);\n  fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });\n  const outPath = path.join(outDir, \"_resource-map.md\");\n  fs.writeFileSync(outPath, parts.join(\"\\n\").trimEnd() + \"\\n\");\n  console.log(\n    `wrote ${path.relative(ROOT, outPath)} (${total} methods, ${groups.size} resources, ${total - unlinked} linked)`,\n  );\n}\n\nfunction validateLauncherSources(lang, cfg, launcher, readme, apiMd) {\n  for (const expected of launcher.readmeAssertions) {\n    if (!readme.includes(expected)) {\n      throw new Error(`${lang}: launcher source assertion missing from ${cfg.readme}: ${expected}`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const balanceLine = apiMd\n    .split(\"\\n\")\n    .find((line) =\u003e line.includes('title=\"get /balance\"'));\n  if (!balanceLine) {\n    throw new Error(`${lang}: GET /balance not found in ${cfg.apiMd}`);\n  }\n  for (const expected of launcher.apiLineAssertions) {\n    if (!balanceLine.includes(expected)) {\n      throw new Error(`${lang}: balance launcher call drifted in ${cfg.apiMd}: ${expected}`);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction launcherCodeBlock(language, code) {\n  return [\n    '\u003cdiv className=\"sdk-launchpad-code\" tabIndex={0}\u003e',\n    \"\",\n    `\\`\\`\\`${language}`,\n    code,\n    \"```\",\n    \"\",\n    \"\u003c/div\u003e\",\n  ].join(\"\\n\");\n}\n\nfunction buildSdkLauncher() {\n  const parts = [\n    BANNER(\"the synced SDK README and api.md artifacts\").trimEnd(),\n    '\u003cdiv id=\"sdk-launchpad\" className=\"sdk-launchpad\"\u003e',\n    '  \u003cp className=\"sdk-launchpad-kicker\"\u003eQUICKSTART\u003c/p\u003e',\n    \"\",\n    \"## Install an SDK and make a request\",\n    \"\",\n    \"[Create an API key](/docs/development/api-fundamentals/authentication-and-security), export it as `TELNYX_API_KEY`, then select a language. Each example performs the read-only [`GET /balance`](/api-reference/billing/get-user-balance-details) request and prints the response.\",\n    \"\",\n    \"\u003cTabs\u003e\",\n  ];\n\n  for (const [lang, launcher] of Object.entries(SDK_LAUNCHER)) {\n    const cfg = LANGS[lang];\n    const readme = fs.readFileSync(path.join(SDK_SHARED, cfg.readme), \"utf8\");\n    const apiMd = fs.readFileSync(path.join(SDK_SHARED, cfg.apiMd), \"utf8\");\n    validateLauncherSources(lang, cfg, launcher, readme, apiMd);\n    const install =\n      typeof launcher.install === \"function\" ? launcher.install(readme) : launcher.install;\n\n    parts.push(\n      `  \u003cTab title=\"${launcher.tabTitle || launcher.title}\" id=\"${lang}\"\u003e`,\n      \"\",\n      \"**Install**\",\n This script projects it into the two places the home\n * directory is rendered from:\n *\n *   home-directory.js   – the SUITE_NAMES blob between the tx-generated\n *                         markers (everything else in that file stays\n *                         hand-managed)\n *   docs/overview.mdx   – the directory tree between the tx-tree markers\n *   custom.css          – per-icon mask classes between the tx-icons markers\n *\n * Contributors edit docs.json and run this script to refresh the generated\n * projections in the same authored PR. CI runs with `--check` and fails closed\n * on drift; it never writes a follow-up commit to the PR branch.\n *\n * Mapping:\n *   products[].product   → suite row (label + icon)\n *   products[].tabs[]    → child rows (label + icon), href = the tab's\n *                          first navigable page\n *   the Home product     → not listed (it IS the directory)\n */\n\nconst fs = require(\"fs\");\nconst path = require(\"path\");\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, \"..\");\nconst DOCS_JSON = path.join(REPO_ROOT, \"docs.json\");\nconst DIRECTORY_JS = path.join(REPO_ROOT, \"home-directory.js\");\nconst OVERVIEW_MDX = path.join(REPO_ROOT, \"docs\", \"overview.mdx\");\n\nconst JS_START = \"  // tx-generated:start — derived from docs.json; run `npm run generate:home-dir`\";\nconst JS_END = \"  // tx-generated:end\";\nconst TREE_START = \"{/* tx-tree:start — derived from docs.json; run `npm run generate:home-dir` */}\";\nconst TREE_END = \"{/* tx-tree:end */}\";\nconst ICONS_START = \"/* tx-icons:start — derived from docs.json; run `npm run generate:home-dir` */\";\nconst ICONS_END = \"/* tx-icons:end */\";\n\nconst CSS_FILE = path.join(REPO_ROOT, \"custom.css\");\nconst ICON_CDN = \"https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/fontawesome/v7.2.0/\";\n\nconst HOME_PRODUCT = \"Home\";\n\n// Products that live under \"Get started\" in the directory instead of the\n// suite tree (Home IS the directory; Other APIs is deliberately unlisted).\nconst TREE_HIDDEN_PRODUCTS = new Set([HOME_PRODUCT, \"Other APIs\"]);\n\n// Hand-curated Get started shortcuts rendered at the top of the directory.\nconst GET_STARTED = [\n  { name: \"Overview\", href: \"/docs/overview\" },\n  { name: \"Development\", href: \"/docs/development\" },\n  { name: \"Other APIs\", href: \"/docs/other-apis\" },\n];\n\n// Directory sections (Cloudflare-style stencil labels). Suites not listed\n// here fall into the trailing \"Products\" bucket so a new suite never\n// disappears from the tree just because nobody assigned it a section.\nconst SUITE_SECTIONS = [\n  { section: \"Agents\", suites: [\"Agent SDK\", \"Agent Tools\", \"AI\", \"Voice\"] },\n  { section: \"Edge Platform\", suites: [\"Compute\", \"Storage\", \"Networking\"] },\n  { section: \"Communications\", suites: [\"Calling\", \"Messaging\", \"Numbers\", \"Wireless\"] },\n];\nconst FALLBACK_SECTION = \"Products\";\n\n// Font Awesome brand glyphs live under brands/ on the icon CDN, not\n// regular/ — Mintlify resolves this automatically for the native nav, but\n// the directory tree builds its own mask URLs (see iconEl in\n// home-directory.js), so the style prefix has to be baked into the data.\nconst BRAND_ICONS = new Set([\"whatsapp\"]);\n\nfunction directoryIcon(name) {\n  if (!name) return null;\n  return BRAND_ICONS.has(name) ? `brands/${name}` : name;\n}\n\n// First navigable page ref under a nav node, depth-first in nav order.\n// Skips external links and file-qualified OpenAPI selectors.\nfunction firstPageRef(node) {\n  if (typeof node === \"string\") {\n    if (node.startsWith(\"http\")) return null;\n    if (/\\s/.test(node)) return null; // \"spec.json GET /path\" selectors\n    return node;\n  }\n  if (Array.isArray(node)) {\n    for (const child of node) {\n      const ref = firstPageRef(child);\n      if (ref) return ref;\n    }\n    return null;\n  }\n  if (node \u0026\u0026 typeof node === \"object\") {\n    return firstPageRef(node.pages) || firstPageRef(node.groups) || null;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nfunction refToHref(ref) {\n  let slug = ref;\n  if (slug.endsWith(\"/index\")) slug = slug.slice(0, -\"/index\".length);\n  return \"/\" + slug;\n}\n\nfunction deriveDirectory(docsJson) {\n  const products = docsJson.navigation?.products || [];\n  const suites = [];\n  for (const product of products) {\n    if (TREE_HIDDEN_PRODUCTS.has(product.product)) continue;\n    const items = [];\n    for (const tab of product.tabs || []) {\n      const ref = firstPageRef(tab);\n      if (!ref) {\n        console.warn(`  ⚠ ${product.product} → ${tab.tab}: no navigable page found — skipped`);\n        continue;\n      }\n      const item = { name: tab.tab };\n      const itemIcon = directoryIcon(tab.icon);\n      if (itemIcon) item.icon = itemIcon;\n      item.href = refToHref(ref);\n      items.push(item);\n    }\n    const suite = { suite: product.product };\n    const suiteIcon = directoryIcon(product.icon);\n    if (suiteIcon) suite.icon = suiteIcon;\n    suite.items = items;\n    suites.push(suite);\n  }\n  return suites;\n}\n\nfunction sectionize(suites) {\n  const byName = new Map(suites.map((s) =\u003e [s.suite, s]));\n  const sections = [];\n  for (const { section, suites: names } of SUITE_SECTIONS) {\n    const present = names.filter((n) =\u003e byName.has(n)).map((n) =\u003e byName.get(n));\n    if (present.length) sections.push({ section, suites: present });\n    for (const n of names) byName.delete(n);\n  }\n  if (byName.size) sections.push({ section: FALLBACK_SECTION, suites: [...byName.values()] });\n  return sections;\n}\n\n// Every product name, hidden ones included — the switcher button must be\n// found (and hidden) on Other APIs pages too.\nfunction renderSuiteNamesBlock(allProductNames, sections) {\n  const lines = [];\n  lines.push(JS_START);\n  lines.push(`  var SUITE_NAMES = ${JSON.stringify(allProductNames)};`);\n  // Use the very same renderer as the desktop MDX, with no duplicated IDs.\n  const html = renderTreeBlock(sections).split('\\n').slice(2, -2).join('\\n')\n    .replace(/className=/g, 'class=').replace(/tabIndex=\\{0\\}/g, 'tabindex=\"0\"')\n    .replace(/ aria-current=\"page\"/g, '');\n  lines.push(`  var DIRECTORY_HTML = ${JSON.stringify(html)};`);\n  lines.push(JS_END);\n  return lines.join(\"\\n\");\n}\n\n// The directory tree as server-rendered MDX/JSX for the mode:custom home\n// page. Same id/classes the injected tree used, so custom.css applies\n// unchanged; icons are per-icon CSS classes (see renderIconClasses) because\n// Mintlify discourages inline style props in MDX.\nfunction iconClass(name) {\n  return \"txi-\" + name.replace(/\\//g, \"-\");\n}\n\nfunction iconSpan(name) {\n  return name ? `\u003cspan className=\"tx-dir-icon ${iconClass(name)}\"\u003e\u003c/span\u003e` : \"\";\n}\n\n// JSX text: escape the few characters MDX/acorn could misparse in labels.\nfunction esc(text) {\n  return text.replace(/\u0026/g, \"\u0026amp;\").replace(/\u003c/g, \"\u0026lt;\").replace(/\u003e/g, \"\u0026gt;\");\n}\n\n// Always-expanded static tree: Mintlify's MDX pipeline drops raw\n// \u003cdetails\u003e/\u003csummary\u003e, and a directory landing reads better fully open\n// anyway (Cloudflare's /directory does not collapse either).\nfunction renderTreeBlock(sections) {\n  const lines = [];\n  lines.push(TREE_START);\n  lines.push('\u003cdiv id=\"tx-directory\" className=\"txd-side\"\u003e');\n  lines.push('  \u003cdiv className=\"tx-dir-label\"\u003eGet started\u003c/div\u003e');\n  for (const item of GET_STARTED) {\n    const current = item.href === \"/docs/overview\" ? ' aria-current=\"page\"' : \"\";\n    lines.push(`  \u003ca className=\"tx-dir-link\" href=${JSON.stringify(item.href)}${current}\u003e\u003cspan\u003e${esc(item.name)}\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/a\u003e`);\n  }\n  for (const section of sections) {\n    lines.push(`  \u003cdiv className=\"tx-dir-label tx-dir-label-gap\"\u003e${esc(section.section)}\u003c/div\u003e`);\n    for (const suite of section.suites) {\n      lines.push('  \u003cdiv className=\"tx-dir-suite\"\u003e');\n      lines.push(`    \u003cdiv className=\"tx-dir-suite-head\" role=\"button\" tabIndex={0} aria-expanded=\"false\"\u003e${iconSpan(suite.icon)}\u003cspan\u003e${esc(suite.suite)}\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/div\u003e`);\n      lines.push(\"    \u003cul\u003e\");\n      for (const item of suite.items) {\n        lines.push(`      \u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=${JSON.stringify(item.href)}\u003e${iconSpan(item.icon)}\u003cspan\u003e${esc(item.name)}\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e`);\n      }\n      lines.push(\"    \u003c/ul\u003e\");\n      lines.push(\"  \u003c/div\u003e\");\n    }\n  }\n  lines.push(\"\u003c/div\u003e\");\n  lines.push(TREE_END);\n  return lines.join(\"\\n\");\n}\n\nfunction renderIconClasses(sections) {\n  const icons = new Set();\n  for (const section of sections) {\n    for (const suite of section.suites) {\n      if (suite.icon) icons.add(suite.icon);\n      for (const item of suite.items) if (item.icon) icons.add(item.icon);\n    }\n  }\n  const lines = [ICONS_START];\n  for (const name of [...icons].sort()) {\n    const style = name.includes(\"/\") ? name : `regular/${name}`;\n    lines.push(\n      `.${iconClass(name)} { -webkit-mask-image: url(\"${ICON_CDN}${style}.svg\"); mask-image: url(\"${ICON_CDN}${style}.svg\"); }`\n    );\n  }\n  lines.push(ICONS_END);\n  return (start === -1 || end === -1 || end \u003c start) {\n    throw new Error(`${file}: tx markers not found — cannot inject generated block`);\n  }\n  return content.slice(0, start) + replacement + file: OVERVIEW_MDX,\n      render: (content) =\u003e\n        replaceBetween(content, TREE_START, TREE_END, renderTreeBlock(sections), \"docs/overview.mdx\"),\n    },\n    {\n      file: CSS_FILE,\n of targets) {\n    const current = fs.readFileSync(file, \"utf8\");\n    const next = render(current);\n    const rel = path.relative(REPO_ROOT, file);\n    if (next === current) {\n      console.log(`  ✓ ${rel} in sync`);\n      continue;\n    }\n    drift = true;\n    if (check) {\n      console.error(`  ✗ ${rel} is stale — run \\`npm run generate:home-dir\\``);\n    } else {\n      fs.writeFileSync(file, next, \"utf8\");\n      console.log(`  → ${rel} regenerated`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (check \u0026\u0026 drift) process.exit(1);\n  console.log(check ? \"Home directory artifacts
